**Status:** Active

Local registry of specifications for this workspace.

| [l1-architecture.md](specifications/l1-architecture.md) ; v1.4.0 INV-9 amended — **retirement is the same rule read backwards**: a verb the surface no longer binds is by definition unbound, so INV-9 governs departure as strictly as arrival, forbidding both extremes — **silent deletion** (breaks scripts and muscle memory with no diagnosis, failing as an unknown command indistinguishable from a typo) and **permanent aliasing** (freezes the grammar around outgrown names and leaves two verbs for one capability, a parity violation of its own); sanctioned shape is a **declared retirement** — off the shipped default surface and out of help/completion, migration path still discoverable, retired invocations resolving to a message naming the replacement, any retained entry point opt-in + marked retired + stating what supersedes it — under the same identity discipline as a distributed artifact's rename (XM-2/HNR-6), an old name never silently re-pointed at a different capability | Layered core+frontends, inward dependencies, hub-and-spoke topology, command parity (INV-1…7); single-deployable **modular monolith** — not network-distributed microservices, sanctioned process boundaries limited to frontend↔core / security-confinement of untrusted code / hub↔spoke & device-replication of the same whole engine (INV-8); INV-9 shipped-surface honesty — a frontend never ships a verb unbound to the core capability it names: unbound commands are hidden/marked unavailable, never "not implemented" stubs (tech-agnostic) ; v1.3.0 INV-10 — **representation isolation at the inward seam**: the domain/core depends only on **shared contract types**; an adapter's **internal representation** (storage schema/row shape, wire DTO, transport-specific form) is **private to the adapter** and mapped to/from contract types at the seam, the domain never naming or depending on it; the **inward** counterpart to INV-2 (which keeps *logic* from leaking *outward* into a frontend) — dependency inversion applied to **data**, the twin of the behavioral trait-seam inversion (a domain module depends on a port trait, never a concrete backend) and of the error-shape boundary (l1-security SEC-11); forbids the classic leak where a persistence table schema or an HTTP DTO quietly *becomes* a domain type, after which the domain cannot compile or unit-test without that backend and silently re-couples to the technology the seam existed to isolate; a representation crossing the seam is mapped never shared (composing INV-8 + the DN-2 seam discipline); realized in l2-crate-topology | Stable | 1 | 1.4.0 |

| [l1-directability.md](specifications/l1-directability.md) | Directability — the office is **directable, not co-driven**: full automation is the foundation (OFF-5/OFF-8), steering is an optional overlay. The pool classifies hires, never refuses them (OA-3). An archetype carries **no authority** — the schema has no vocabulary for permissions/budget/autonomy (OA-4, SEC-10); third-party archetype prose is agent instruction and is vetted before admission (OA-5). scheduled read-only intelligence digest — narrative + activity/type/confidence analytics, composing scheduler + dashboard (MI-5); salience-gated confidence-honest capture policy with actor attribution, caller-declared explicit expiry, and capture-time cross-reference (MI-6); end-of-run procedural distillation of a bounded agentic run into one structured procedure memory (MI-7); structured comparison-operator filter predicate — eq/ne/ordering/membership/containment combinable via AND/OR/NOT (MI-8); reversible lifecycle states active/paused/archived distinct from delete with audited transitions, prune-protective over decay (MI-9); capture-time normalization of relative→absolute time against the observation instant (MI-10); caller capture directives include/exclude/custom-instructions steering extraction without lowering the honesty floor (MI-11); raw-verbatim vs inferred-LLM capture modes as a local-first escape hatch (MI-12); MI-13 experience reuse — recall-before-acting gated reuse (active counterpart to MI-7 capture): before a costly/repeatable action, query typed success/failure/insight quality-scored prior experiences (MI-2/MI-8 filters); a high-quality prior success MAY be reused directly (short-circuit) only when a similarity+score+freshness gate passes, a failure surfaces as avoid-signal, an insight injects as guidance; every outcome captured back typed+scored (MI-6/MI-7); four guards — gated reuse, independent read/write, reused-not-re-derived attribution (MI-1), retained authority gate (SEC-9/SEC-10); case-based reasoning over the substrate (§4.12); nodus: reuse = RUN(@macro), gating host-side on StorageProvider (no nodus invariant); subject-of-memory lens about-user vs about-agent-self (MI-6 ext); ideas-to-adopt no-duplication ledger vs l2-memory-store §4.6/§4.7/§4.11–4.16 (cross-agent ACL/access-log, open-vocabulary auto-categories, declarative retention policy dispositioned as refinement candidates to memory-model/security/telemetry) + nodus StorageProvider/policy-config/error-taxonomy/operator-gap/macro-learning relevance mapping; v1.1.0 MI-14 **anchored neighbourhood recall** — a third mode beside similarity and temporal-window: given an anchor record (or a query top hit), return the records around it in time to a declared depth before/after, **interleaving every record kind** in one chronological sequence; explicitly not servable by re-ranking, since similarity returns the one thing a neighbourhood cannot contain (more records like the anchor, from unrelated moments); bounded depth, per-item kind + signed offset, anchor always marked; serves reconstruction (an isolated hit is unreadable without the work around it) and honest attribution (a decision without the discussion that produced it is a fact stripped of its reason) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |

| [l1-work-convergence.md](specifications/l1-work-convergence.md) ; v1.2.0 CONV-9 added - a **decision-shaped unit** converges like any other and is not a second queue: work whose product is a decision rather than a deliverable lands on the same board under the same rules, honouring CONV-3's one-kind-of-work-unit **by construction** (a card whose completion criterion is a recorded answer, not a parallel representation with its own lifecycle), declaring its relation from the same closed CONV-2 set, and **may not carry a deliverable as its product** - a unit that produced one has changed kind and the board shows the change; closes the loophole of a planning phase run off-board because it is not-work-yet, which is CONV-1's shadow work wearing a phase name | Convergence contract — the office runs **all** activity through one legible surface, the Kanban board; no shadow work (CONV-1). fidelity-honest lossy-declared mapping — unmappable fields surfaced not silently dropped, comments folded with author/date (WI-7); asset/reference rewriting — embedded attachment/image URLs re-hosted or authenticated with a skip hatch, no dangling/credential-leaking external link (WI-8); interactive + headless parity, headless flags = the embeddability contract (WI-9, INV-3); converges as materialize cards through triage never a shadow queue, live-tracker pull consent-gated + credential-scoped egress while local-file import needs none, an authorized onboarding action not a silent sync (WI-10, composes l1-work-convergence CONV-3 + l1-security + SUP-3); ideas-to-adopt mapping (source-adapter migration, normalized result, dedup/resolve reconciliation, status-type inference) + nodus disposition Reuse behind host provider seams (LP-2), no new nodus invariant (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-work-liveness.md](specifications/l1-work-liveness.md) ; v1.3.0 WL-11 - **a guard that blocks completion measures semantic progress and carries a bounded release**: WL-8 classified a live-but-silent run, but the inverse mechanism (a guard refusing to let an actor *stop* while obligations remain) has its own failure mode; progress is read from **resolved obligation state, never artifact bytes** (a byte-diff guard counts a reflowed line, an added comment, or the recorder rewriting its own metadata as progress, re-arming forever so its release can only fire for an actor doing literally nothing - the one case least in need of it), the release is **bounded and diagnostic** after a declared number of consecutive no-progress blocks (an actor that is trying and failing is who an unbounded guard traps hardest), the budget is **per-actor** with serialized state and clearance on obligation disappearance, and the guard **observes, never executes** (a stop-time guard that ran its own checks would make stopping produce effects and turn every abandoned attempt into a side effect); each block states an actionable next move | Work liveness & ownership — keeps autonomously-executed work units alive, single-owned, and recoverable: exclusive atomic claim with conflict-not-clobber release (WL-1/WL-2, no double-work); affirmative liveness contract — every owned non-terminal unit always has a next-move from a closed typed set (active run · queued wake · deferred monitor · pending interaction/approval · human owner · live blocker-leaf · open recovery action) or is surfaced stalled (WL-3); single active run + wake coalescing realizing the de-dup the scheduler model deferred (WL-4); owner-preserving stranded-work reconciliation distinct from run-resume via a reap/resume/reconcile sweep (WL-5); bounded auto→explicit→human recovery ladder (WL-6) with a typed durable recovery action — comment-is-evidence-not-path (WL-7); silence-is-suspect liveness watchdog that classifies a wedged-but-alive run without killing it (WL-8); idempotent exact-once fan-out keyed by (source, approved revision) (WL-9); sits beneath orchestration delegation and above a single execution-graph run; composes with l1-kanban-model / l1-doctor / l1-operational-health; §4.3 concrete WL-1 realization — negated compare-and-set (transition to active iff not-active, race-free) — plus cooperative external preemption of a healthy run (STOP/PAUSE/RESUME on a per-unit channel, producer/consumer decoupled) with terminal-state-plus-short-TTL so a crashed executor's claim self-expires into reap (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.3.0 |

| [l1-orchestration.md](specifications/l1-orchestration.md) ; v1.3.0 ORC-13/14 added - **ending a dispatch's bookkeeping is a declared disposition**: a re-delegation returns the unit to the pool (ORC-4), a **handoff release** ends orchestration tracking and authority over the dispatch WITHOUT signaling or tearing down the delegate's still-running process, which continues as an explicitly **unsupervised** entity outside the orchestration's lifecycle, recorded as such never silently (ORC-13); the canonical event carries both a human-legible message and machine-legible typed fields as first-class content, neither a lossy reparse of the other (LL-2); each projection consumer-shaped — human scannable/attributed/folded, machine complete/queryable, agent compact/payload-free — over a canonical event that stays complete (LL-3); overload bounded by construction via three composable controls, severity/verbosity gating × payload policy (descriptor/excerpt/full) × compaction (fold repeats to ×N, window the tail), conservative defaults with full fidelity opt-in, no reader handed a firehose by default (LL-4); honest reduction — every truncation/fold/omission/sample flagged with its magnitude (original size, repeat count, sample rate), a reduced record never masquerades as complete so neither human nor AI mistakes an excerpt for the whole (LL-5); stable attribution (component/severity/category/correlation-id/acting-role) for filterability, the mechanism that makes economy achievable — narrow rather than drink the firehose (LL-6); secret-safety re-asserted at every projection not just the sink, widening visibility never widens secret exposure, sensitive sources denylistable from capture (LL-7); AI-consumer economy — a log fed back into an agent's own context is the compact payload-free budget-bounded projection never the raw firehose, reading its own logs must not evict working context (LL-8, composes context-budget + cache-stable-context); stable self-describing versioned namespaced schema with a migration path so a machine/AI parses without human explanation and a schema change is a migration not a silent break (LL-9); the legibility/economy contract over every observation channel (l1-diagnostic-log / semantic trace / l1-operational-health / l1-dashboard / l1-telemetry), composing l1-security + l1-context-provenance + l1-inner-monologue; a **closed, uniform point-kind taxonomy** — *observe* (react, cannot change outcome) / *transform* (value→value pipeline) / *provide* (core selects one implementation) / *contribute* (add an invocable surface) / *decide* (answer/short-circuit a decision) — used by **every aspect** (office, board, automation, memory, wiki, model serving, roles, navigation/fabric, version-control, security, CLI), no per-subsystem bespoke mechanism (EP-2/EP-8); **deterministic composition under declared precedence** — observe fan-out / transform pipeline / provide selection / decide first-non-abstaining / contribute name-collision rule — never load-order-accidental (EP-4); **versioned seam contracts** with pre-activation refusal of incompatible targets (EP-5); **fail-isolated** contributions under declared per-kind failure policies, kernel survives a bad plugin (EP-6); least-privilege, declared, grant-gated point reach (EP-7); **lazy activation** (installed-but-unused costs nothing) + inspectable/auditable live wiring (EP-9); microkernel/contribution-point best-practice synthesis; composes l1-extensions/l2-plugin-hooks(one realization of observe/decide)/l1-architecture/l1-security; main-only host architecture concept (tech-agnostic)  ; v1.1.0 adds the three rules governing a contribution's **presence** rather than its invocation: EP-10 core-drawn attribution marker a contribution cannot compose away + interrupt ceiling (may interrupt work, never a decision *about the session itself*); EP-11 reserved core namespace, contributor identity owns its qualified names so no shadowing *whatever the core adds later*; EP-12 the core registers its own implementations through the published seam — no privileged door, because a contract only third parties exercise is one nobody notices has become insufficient  ; v1.3.0 EP-14 — **contributed metadata is validated and detached at the seam, and so is what a contribution returns**: EP-5 versions the seam's shape and EP-6 contains a contribution's failure, but nothing governed the contributor-controlled **data** crossing it in either direction; a descriptor validated then mutated in place leaves the core rendering text no check saw, and an unchecked return reaches a renderer or a wire wearing the core's authority so the defect is attributed to the core; validation refuses and names, never silently repairs, and bounds belong at entry rather than at serialization. EP-4 amended: a *contribute* collision rule MAY resolve by **scope**, where the narrower entry **shadows** rather than displaces — the shadowed one stays registered, stays reachable by qualified identity, and returns when the scope ends, and the rule must state which it means | Stable | 1 | 1.3.0 |

One decision point per observable behavior (SP-1); derived facts consumed, never recomputed — defective **while still agreeing** (SP-2); divergence as a tracked finding inventory (SP-3); **repaid = copies deleted + deletion pinned + adversarial fixture + every consumer registered** (SP-4); append-only tombstones, shrink-only debt (SP-5); parity proven by one corpus through each surface's **real** projection (SP-6); a new consumer registers before it ships (SP-7); legitimate difference named with its reason (SP-8); **extract before the second implementation exists** (SP-9); **converge first, correct second** (SP-10); shared action catalog with declared locus, where an unexposed action is declared rather than merely absent (SP-11)  ; v1.1.0 SP-11 gains a fourth **installation** locus — verbs acting on the product's own installation run to completion without a session and belong on no session-scoped surface, a class the semantic/client-local/host-only triad had no member for and which was therefore being modelled as semantic; SP-12 splits a catalog entry into a **projectable face and an executable one with a per-member declaration**, since both defaults fail (a wholly serializable entry ships host handles across a wire, a wholly opaque one forces every remote surface to restate the catalog — the fork SP-11 prevents, reintroduced at the process boundary); SP-13 separates **unrecognized from unavailable** — resolution has three outcomes, and folding the middle one removes a surface's ability to fall through on input that was never a command | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |
| [l1-launch-handoff.md](specifications/l1-launch-handoff.md) | Launch handoff — how a process becomes a running surface, and the boundary between the part of an invocation the launcher owns and the part it must not read. Answers the ordering problem an extensible command surface creates: a launcher that resolves a verb must know the verb set, the verb set exists only after extensions load, and loading them on every invocation defeats lazy activation — resolved not by a faster loader but by a **narrower launcher**. The launcher's grammar is closed, small, self-owned, and ends at the first token it does not own, the remainder carried verbatim (LH-1); the invocation is a **provided fact, never an ambient read**, so an embedding host with no command line supplies an empty one and the embedded and launched paths are the same path rather than two that agree until one is tested (LH-2); whoever owns a grammar owns its help (LH-3); an invocation naming no composition boots the declared default, which is **also** addressable by name so a script never depends on the default not changing (LH-4); the launcher **never resolves a contributed name** (LH-5); a pre-composition answer — usage, version, completion — is served from a built artifact that **declares its own invalidation** rather than a hand-maintained copy (LH-6); launcher, composition, and application failures are three distinguishable outcomes and a usage failure starts no lifecycle (LH-7); startup **commits once** with post-startup work gated behind it so a boot failure is not displaced by the first symptom of work that should never have begun (LH-8); the application **requests** exit and the launcher performs it bounded (LH-9); one entry procedure for every surface, surfaces differing by composition alone (LH-10) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

regression-bounded + held-out-honest + train/eval-disjoint + reward-hacking-guarded acceptance (HX-8, also formalizes the reward-hacking-filter mechanic); budgeted terminating search (HX-9); durable concurrency-safe resumable observable frontier (HX-10); composes l1-harness-engineering (per-candidate evolution) / l1-agent-coevaluation (scoring+comparability) / l1-evaluation-suites (grading) / l1-attestation (hashing) / l1-nodus-environment (graded-run substrate NE-12) — adds the search+frontier layer none owned (tech-agnostic); HX-11 budget-normalized evaluation & mutation-invariant metric — when candidates in one search differ in resource-affecting dimensions their evaluations MUST be budget-comparable: preferred design is budget normalization (the evaluation contract fixes the resource budget — wall-clock/compute/tokens/trials — not the workload, every candidate measured on outcome-within-fixed-budget) so radically heterogeneous candidates compete directly, efficiency is absorbed into the outcome rather than hand-weighed as a second axis, and the optimum is honestly platform-relative (budget + platform recorded with every score, composing MR-14); differing budgets partition the frontier (HX-7, sharpening ACE-5's a-longer-budget-is-a-different-task row into a design principle); and the scoring metric MUST be mutation-invariant — chosen to remain meaningful across the entire mutable candidate space, a mutation never able to change what the metric measures (criteria-drift sibling of loop-governance oracle-ownership); v1.2.0 HX-12 — **offline acceptance is necessary, never sufficient**: HX-8's held-out regression-bounded acceptance admits a candidate to the archive but may not by itself promote it to the default for live work; an accepted candidate reaches real work behind a named, instantly revertible switch at a declared exposure fraction with deterministic assignment and a reserved holdout on the prior default, bounding a regression the held-out set could not see to the exposed fraction and making it reversible without shipping; the reason is structural rather than a matter of gate strength — the held-out set is a *sample of tasks* while live work is a *distribution that moves*, so a candidate promoted straight to 100% on a held-out win has been validated against the past and deployed against the future; exposure fraction, guardrails, and stopping rule declared before exposure and frozen for its lifetime (the live analog of HX-8 held-out honesty and HX-11 criteria-immutability) | Stable | 1 | 1.2.1 |
| [l1-harness-composition.md](specifications/l1-harness-composition.md) | Harness composition — the right-sizing / continuous-justification / anti-bloat *trim* counterpart to harness-engineering's *grow*: minimal justified composition, bloat is a first-class defect not neutral surplus (HC-1); component = named capability-gap compensation, no nameable gap ⇒ unjustified (HC-2); continuous re-justification — prune when the model or host closes the gap, justification is not one-time (HC-3); dedup against host-native + existing components, host-supersedes-custom ⇒ remove naming the native feature (HC-4); anti-sprawl ≥N-axis justification gate for specialists (HC-5, = roles ROL-9); bounded complexity — capped delegation depth + scale-proportional component count (HC-6); right-size to the subject from a trait-selected catalog, not a one-size maximal set (HC-7); deferred-with-trigger not speculative (HC-8); lifecycle disposition keep/update/add/remove with reasons + safe reversible archive-then-remove (HC-9); observable/auditable composition, drift-to-bloat detectable (HC-10); composes harness-engineering/harness-optimization/agent-coevaluation/roles/orchestration/extensions/office-model; two supersession sources (model-improves, host-adds-native) invisible unless actively checked (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-solution-frugality.md](specifications/l1-solution-frugality.md) | Solution frugality — the output-side anti-over-engineering discipline (sibling to harness-composition's tooling-side right-sizing; the subject is the produced deliverable code, not the agent's harness): comprehension precedes frugality — a minimal diff produced without understanding the change is a confident wrong fix, the discipline shortens the solution never the reading (FR-1); the reuse-before-build ladder, first-hold-wins — need-to-exist?/reuse-in-codebase/language-stdlib/native-platform/installed-dependency/one-expression/minimum-new-code (FR-2); the hard negligence floor — frugality never cuts trust-boundary validation / data-loss error handling / security / accessibility / requested scope / physical calibration, below it "less" is a defect (FR-3); root-cause locality — one guard at the shared point, not per-caller (FR-4); tunable non-self-elevated intensity off/advisory/enforced/aggressive, human-set per office/session, never model-elevated (FR-5, composes SEC-10); disclosed simplification with a named ceiling + upgrade trigger, harvestable debt ledger, no-trigger rot flag, undisclosed cut = defect (FR-6, composes operational-ledger + self-improvement); complexity-only scoped non-mutating frugality review/audit with a closed finding vocabulary delete/stdlib-covered/native-covered/speculative-abstraction/shrinkable, produces a delete-list never applies it (FR-7); output economy — unrequested justification prose is complexity, requested reports exempt (FR-8); proportional verification — one runnable check for non-trivial logic, composes quality-standards' mandatory-test gate, never licenses skipping a required test (FR-9); measured-not-asserted with an honest counterfactual — never fabricate a per-instance savings number, the counted debt ledger is the only real per-project figure (FR-10); projects onto nodus as macro-reuse (RUN(@macro)) + a frugality-lint family over authored workflows, no new language primitive (§4.6) (tech-agnostic); v1.0.1 links `l1-change-containment` as the independent edit-footprint axis (FR-7 delete-findings scheduled separately CTN-3; FR-4 root-cause a containment warrant not creep; FR-1 comprehension extends to removal CTN-2) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.1 |
| [l1-change-containment.md](specifications/l1-change-containment.md) ; v1.2.0 CTN-12 added - the **wide mechanical change** exception: a single mechanical transformation whose call sites number in the hundreds cannot be made both correct and contained, and splitting it into contained pieces yields pieces that do not build; sanctioned shape is **expand -> migrate -> contract** (new form beside the old, call sites migrated in blast-radius-sized batches each leaving the system green, old form removed in a final unit gated on every batch), the exception **declared not assumed** because it is the most available excuse for an unwarranted hunk (CTN-1 applies unchanged within each batch), and where a batch cannot stand green alone the batches share an integration line gated by one explicit **verify** unit | Change containment — the **edit-footprint** discipline, the diff-side counterpart to solution frugality (which bounds solution *size* and has no notion of an edit's entitlement; the two axes fail independently — §4.6 four-cell table). Every changed line carries one of exactly three warrants — realization / correctness-necessity (root cause, updated caller, gate-required test, sibling path) / self-repair of an orphan this change created — with no fourth; "it was better this way" is not a warrant (CTN-1). **Comprehension gate on removal**: nothing whose purpose was never established — code, branch, config value, or *comment* — may be deleted, rewritten, or condensed, at any change size; apparent redundancy is evidence of missing comprehension, not of dead code, and "no static references" is a lookup result not a purpose (CTN-2, extends FR-1 to the removal side). Foreign dead code is **reported, never removed in the same change**; orphans the change itself created are cleaned up in it — the line is authorship of the orphaning, not the code's state (CTN-3). Local idiom outranks personal preference, an explicit standard outranks both, and reconciling a divergence is its own change (CTN-4). Mechanical transformation never bundled with semantic change — the direct cause of unreviewable diffs and lost history legibility; lands on the VC-3 card-aligned commit boundary (CTN-5). Expansion **declared and warranted**, with silent widening *and* correctness-narrowing both defects — containment governs disclosure, never timidity (CTN-6). Declining to edit is never declining to report: every deferred observation filed as an actionable finding, since acquiring knowledge and discarding it is worse than the drive-by edit (CTN-7). A distinct **non-mutating** containment review with a closed vocabulary — unwarranted-hunk / style-drift / foreign-cleanup / bundled-mechanical / comprehension-gap / undeclared-expansion — that must not flag warranted root-cause or gate-test hunks (CTN-8). Measured on the diff (unwarranted-hunk share, off-surface files, mechanical-line ratio, blind removals, deferral discipline), never asserted, no fabricated per-change savings figure (CTN-9). Uniform rules, proportional ceremony (CTN-10). **nodus projection, no new primitive**: dual-representation AST-equality makes the mechanical/semantic split *decidable* for authored workflows (bundled-mechanical becomes a check, not a heuristic), plus a containment lint family and reuse of declared effect surfaces as mechanical rather than instructed enforcement (§4.8). Composes solution-frugality / development-workflow (DW-4) / review-checkpoint (RC-7) / version-control (VC-3) / code-intelligence / operational-ledger / intent-resolution (IR-3) / pattern-codification / practice-analytics; v1.1.0 CTN-11 **a declared edit boundary is enforced by mechanism, blocks rather than warns, and is never self-relaxed** — the mechanical floor under a discipline that was entirely self-applied: for high-cost work the principal declares which paths the work may write to, enforced at the write gate rather than by instructing the producer (instruction being the enforcement that already failed); narrowable by the producer, widenable only by the principal (SEC-10 / AG-8), so the constrained actor cannot dissolve its own constraint; scoped and announced; explicitly a floor never a substitute — staying inside the path does not make an unwarranted hunk warranted (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.2.0 |

| [l1-fidelity-variants.md](specifications/l1-fidelity-variants.md) | Fidelity variants — the axis the runtime's degradation discipline **explicitly declines**: MR-15 trades speed for capacity and MUST NOT alter outputs, classifying a result-altering configuration as *a different configuration*, and that class had no owner. **A fidelity variant changes the output; a placement tier does not**, and conflating them lets a quality regression enter through the door reserved for changes promised not to alter anything (FV-1); the variant set is declared, ordered, and profiled with its resource cost and quality effect, since an undeclared "fast mode" is a second product shipped without a name (FV-2); selection is explicit or policy-driven and **never opportunistic**, because silent substitution yields consecutive results whose difference has no recorded cause — indistinguishable to a user from the system becoming unreliable (FV-3); the variant **travels with the artifact** as a recipe field, since an artifact whose fidelity is unrecorded will be compared anyway (FV-4); **measurement is within-variant**, generalizing MB-5 (which already folds quantization into the identity of the model under test) to every variant-bearing capability and every instrument, with mixed-variant comparisons reported invalid rather than averaged (FV-5); a **declared floor** below which the honest answer is a stated refusal with the shortfall, because a chain of individually reasonable reductions otherwise terminates in output that is cheap and useless with nothing objecting (FV-6); **no silent upgrade either** — spending budget the requester did not authorize is the same defect pointed the other way (FV-7); the quality effect **measured and sliced**, never assumed monotone, since lower fidelity typically collapses one slice while barely moving the mean, and `UNKNOWN` is a first-class value rather than an omission (FV-8); a **named reference variant** anchoring the axis, without which "the fast one" and "the good one" drift into unrelated products with no common yardstick (FV-9). CE-10's select-cheap-produce-once is the worked special case, referenced not restated. **nodus projection, no new primitive**: the `§config` surface declares the choice with a stale id resolving to the reference, the run-record annotation carries it, and the environment contract's comparability partition (profile/budget/measure) already expresses the within-variant rule. Composes model-runtime (MR-7/MR-15/MR-16) / competitive-execution (CE-10) / model-benchmarking (MB-5) / agent-coevaluation (ACE-4/ACE-5) / reproduction-recipe (RR-2) / outcome-attributed-cost / usage-allowance (UA-8) / generation-shaping (GS-4) / outcome-confidence (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-model-runtime.md](specifications/l1-model-runtime.md) | Local model runtime — lifecycle substrate routing schedules onto: local-first on-device serving with explicit remote opt-in (MR-1); provider-abstracted backend contract load/generate-stream/embed/describe/unload (MR-2); content-addressed immutable-blob model store with manifest digest pinning + dedup (MR-3, reuses l1-file-management); verifiable resumable named registry acquisition + acquire-on-run + export (MR-4); portable declarative model definition base+params+template+system+adapters built without copying weights (MR-5); explicit load/unload lifecycle with idle/LRU eviction + resident-model introspection (MR-6); fit-gated hardware scheduling — feasibility gate with placement full-accel/partial-offload/CPU + shortfall report, scoring delegated to router (MR-7); streaming inference contract + industry-compatible surface (MR-8); full catalog management list/show/copy/remove with blob GC (MR-9); managed observable background server start/stop/status + logs + health (MR-10); thin CLI/SDK clients over the server, single source of truth, graceful offline degradation (MR-11); loose-or-digest reproducible references with served-digest recording (MR-12); multi-device placement — feasibility gate over an accelerator set, split across devices before CPU fallback, per-device residency/shortfall, mixture-of-expert footprint sizing (resident-total vs active-per-token) (MR-13); calibrated honest estimates — figures labeled estimate-vs-measurement, recalibrated by recorded (model,placement,hardware) residency/throughput, community-measured corpus as opt-in telemetry-gated extension (MR-14); v1.2.0 MR-15 **degrade before refusing** — where a model does not fit the fastest placement the runtime MUST consider a named degradation tier trading throughput for capacity before refusing under the MR-7 fit gate, refusal becoming the last answer rather than the first, because on a local-first system running on hardware the user already owns "your machine cannot run this" is far more often a failure of the runtime than a fact about the machine and a runtime that only refuses is unusable on exactly the modest hardware it exists to serve; three obligations — the tier's cost **stated before the run** as a calibrated basis-carrying estimate (MR-14) so a slow run is chosen rather than discovered, the tier **recorded on the run** so a slow result is attributable to the placement instead of mistaken for a model or prompt regression, and a tier **changing speed never outputs** (a placement that alters results is a different configuration and therefore a reproduction-recipe field, RR-2 ambient layer, not an invisible operational choice); v1.3.0 MR-16 **feasibility is a running property, not a load-time verdict** — MR-7 answers *can this start here*, not *will it still fit an hour in*: the **load transient** can peak near twice the resident footprint (a model that fits once resident may fail while arriving, so the gate reasons about the peak and the loading path honours the approved figure); **resident ≠ active**, so a single size scalar mis-predicts memory and throughput in opposite directions for architectures where only a fraction of parameters participate per step; and **demand grows with the working context**, per-sequence attention state growing with the sequence at an architecture-declared rate, so a run that started comfortably crosses the boundary later. Estimates are driven by declared resource characteristics riding the MR-5 portable definition (absent characteristic = degraded confidence per MR-14, never a zero), and a mid-run crossing is a **managed transition** — reduce the working set → named degradation tier (MR-15) → honest stop with the shortfall — recorded on the run, since an unrecorded mid-run placement change is the misattribution MR-15 exists to prevent (tech-agnostic); v1.3.1 links `l1-fidelity-variants` as the owner of the class MR-15 explicitly hands off — a result-altering configuration is not a degradation tier | Stable | 1 | 1.3.1 |
| [l1-agent-tool-ergonomics.md](specifications/l1-agent-tool-ergonomics.md) ; v1.4.0 ATE-14 — a diagnostic carries its **stable code, exact subject, measured evidence, and enumerated admissible fixes**, because an agent handed only a description invents a fix, applies several at once, and cannot tell which one moved the number; where the diagnostic already **computed** a corrective value the agent uses it verbatim rather than estimating its own (the ATE-11 copy-verbatim discipline applied to a computed correction), and repairs go one diagnosed change per round with re-measurement between, since a batch against a shared measurement leaves the round unattributable exactly when the loop must decide whether it is converging (LG-12) | Agent tool ergonomics — design discipline for agent-facing tool surfaces (MCP/extension/in-session) that agents actually use well, won by shaping tool I/O around existing agent behavior not by instructing the agent: sufficiency-to-stop as the design target — output complete enough the agent doesn't fall back to manual discovery (ATE-1); recoverable conditions return success-shaped guidance, hard/isError reserved for stop-trying — early false errors teach session-wide abandonment (ATE-2); absence as the unmissable signal — empty tool list + inactive notice over tools that all fail (ATE-3); adapt-the-tool-to-the-agent over instruct-the-agent — influence channels (descriptions/init instructions) are low-salience and don't move tool choice (ATE-4); output budget monotonic in workload — larger tier never smaller per-item budget (ATE-5); explicit per-item staleness signaling (ATE-6, general form of CI-3); never steer to a manual fallback (ATE-7); lean surface by measured pick-rate folding rare tools inline (ATE-8, reuses TC-7); validate by A/B ablation on the floor model — affordance that lands on the weakest model generalizes up (ATE-9); ideas-to-adopt + nodus error-taxonomy/sufficiency/floor-model relevance; per-call intent declaration on mutating tools, schema-level and ordered before payload (ATE-10); opaque reference passing with host-side hydration, copy-verbatim identifier discipline (ATE-11); host-side overflow guard for uncontrolled tool results — a result exceeding a declared host bound is offloaded whole to durable local storage and replaced by a bounded navigable reference (retrievable/searchable on demand), never blowing the window nor silently truncated, the lossless trace-recorded scoped exception to ATE-7 for third-party tools the host cannot redesign, tool-output dual of RD-1, composing ATE-11 (ATE-12, §4.5); v1.3.0 ATE-13 **unresolved address ≠ wrong invocation form** — opposite corrective actions (find another capability vs correct a call to one already found), so collapsing both into "unknown tool" makes an agent abandon a reachable capability (the ATE-1 manual-fallback failure one step earlier); the wrong-form rejection enumerates the forms the target does offer (one corrected call, not a search), both success-shaped per ATE-2, neither steering to a manual fallback per ATE-7; also an ATE-8 coverage signal — a target never invoked in an offered form is misadvertised, not misunderstood (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.4.0 |
| [l1-tool-call-transport.md](specifications/l1-tool-call-transport.md) | Tool-call transport — the wire seam by which a logical tool invocation crosses the agent↔model boundary and the reply is decoded back: logical/wire separation, a tool call/result defined independent of transport with at least a native structured channel and a text-encoded protocol (TCT-1); deterministic pure bidirectional encode/decode round-trip holding no loop state (TCT-2); capability-detected per-turn selection preferring native, encoded as the universal fallback so a no-native-tool-API backend is never a dead end, explicit recorded override (TCT-3); single tool-schema source (tool-composition TC-2) rendered transport-idiomatically — native tool list vs injected description block, never double-authored (TCT-4); reasoning isolation — a thought span lifted out before call parsing so private reasoning is never mis-parsed as intent (TCT-5); multi-call fidelity — zero/one/many calls per turn decoded in order, never dropped/merged/reordered, dispatch order delegated to the tool DAG (TCT-6); streaming-safe incremental decode — partial protocol tokens buffered not flashed, cancellation leaves no half-parsed call (TCT-7); typed recoverable malformed-call containment re-injected for output-contract repair-retry under the generation budget, never crash/silent no-op (TCT-8); result re-injection symmetry across transports and mid-session transport switches (TCT-9); provenance-gated decode — only the model's own channel decoded, an encoded look-alike in untrusted retrieved/tool/user content is inert (TCT-10, reuses context-provenance CP-1/CP-2); ideas-to-adopt mapping (native-vs-prompted tool-calling, symmetric pre/postprocess, streaming partial-token tolerance) + nodus disposition Reuse behind ModelProvider LP-2, no new nodus invariant (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

| [l1-completion-verification.md](specifications/l1-completion-verification.md) ; v1.5.0 CMP-11 — **producing the evidence is not examining it**: where a check's output is an artifact somebody must look at, the claim states what was looked for and what was seen, since a file's existence and its path in the report are indistinguishable from an inspection that never happened; the medium must carry the property (a still frame cannot evidence a transition) and the observation must enter at the claim's own layer (synthesizing an event downstream of the mechanism under test cannot prove the mechanism fired) ; v1.3.0 CMP-8 **evidence does not travel upward - a roll-up re-executes, it does not inherit**: a parent/branch/orchestrator re-runs the child's own authoritative checks rather than reading its recorded status (which reproduces what was true in the child's environment before every later change, including the parent's own integration), a child whose oracle no longer passes **returns to unmet**, and an explicitly handed-off required outcome is **terminal non-success** that never aggregates into a complete parent at any level (complete siblings + one handoff = a handoff) - extending CMP-3 from the delegate's *word* to the delegate's *record*; CMP-9 **evidence names the environment it was produced in** (interpreter, location, name-resolution context, observed termination), so a material difference on re-execution is *unverified - environment differs*, a mismatch to resolve rather than a pass to accept - the environmental axis of CMP-1 temporal freshness (stale in *place* as well as stale in *time*) ; v1.4.0 CMP-10 added - a completion requiring a **counterparty** is not satisfied by the actor's own contribution: CMP-1...CMP-9 all govern claims about work the actor did, and a distinct class escapes every one of them - an outcome whose definition requires a second party's participation (a human decision, an approval, an interview answer, an external acknowledgement) where the actor supplies that party's side itself; every prior invariant passes because the work was real, the evidence fresh, the check proving and the report honest - what was fabricated is the **participant**, not the report; the counterparty's contribution must be **present and attributable**, absence yields *not complete* rather than complete-with-an-inferred-answer, and the detectable shape is named because it is what occurs: **a record with one participant, and an outcome attributed to nobody** | Completion verification — the **work-state** member of the grounding/honesty family (beside claim-verification for *facts* and tool-receipts for *actions*): a claim that work is complete/passing/fixed/succeeded needs **fresh same-turn evidence** from the authoritative check (test output+exit, build exit, original symptom re-run), else the *actual verified* state is reported — extrapolation/prior-run/"should pass"/confidence/partial-check is not evidence (CMP-1); the claim **names its check** (command+full-output+failure-count, reconstructable/falsifiable), an unrunnable check ⇒ `unverified` not asserted (CMP-2, composes OI-8); **delegated success is verified independently** against an observable artifact the subordinate did not author the report of (actual diff/output/RunResult), never relayed on the subordinate's word — a self-report is a signal to verify not a verdict to relay (CMP-3, the cross-actor case tool-receipts TR-8 leaves open); the **check proves the claim not a weaker proxy** — linter≠build, build≠tests, one-pass≠regression-fixed (red-green), green-suite≠requirements-met (checked line-by-line, gaps reported) (CMP-4); **no unverified completion status is surfaced as done** on any surface (status line/report/hand-off/commit-PR/orchestrator roll-up), unverified is labeled never rendered as done (CMP-5); closes the highest-frequency agent dishonesty — "done!" without running the check, and relaying a sub-agent's unverified "success" upward — sitting in the gap between claim-verification (facts) and tool-receipts (actions); nodus-relevance needs no new NL invariant — RunResult/@err/!!/@test already ground outcomes, CMP is the host-side "don't lie about the RunResult" consumption discipline; v1.1.0 CMP-6 — a probabilistic outcome estimate is **not evidence in either direction**: however well calibrated it may never satisfy/replace/weaken/shorten/waive a required check, and a check's absence is never excused by a high estimate; composition is one-way (evidence decides *done*, an estimate decides *how hard to look and whom to involve*), so a low estimate may demand more checking than the minimum while a high one may never demand less; the converse is closed too — a passing check does not license suppressing a low estimate from the record or hand-off, since the estimate's value is flagging the gaps no check was written for; both travel with the outcome, neither overwrites the other; v1.2.0 CMP-7 **a dismissal is a claim and needs the same evidence** — the symmetric assertion that a failure is *not this work's* ("pre-existing", "unrelated", "flaky", "fails on main too") now requires the check run against the **baseline** with output shown: reproduces there = pre-existing, does not reproduce there = **yours** whatever the reasoning suggests, baseline unrunnable = *unverified, may or may not be related* carried as a risk; demanding proof to say *done* while accepting confidence to say *not mine* leaves the cheapest escape hatch open and hides real regressions when they are cheapest to find (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.5.0 |

SP-3…SP-8/SP-10 realized as a **finding inventory**, two one-way **ledgers**, and a **conformance corpus** shaped as a shared fixture library plus a harness each surface runs in its own test target — the shape forced by the desktop shell's detached build workspace and the shape SP-7 asks for independently. | [l2-ui-module-topology.md](specifications/l2-ui-module-topology.md) | Module decomposition of `packages/ui` — the TypeScript twin of l2-crate-topology, facing a language with no compile-time module boundary inside a package. Four tiers (composition root → shell → surfaces → shared) with a total one-way import order; UMT-1…UMT-7: tier order with no lateral surface-to-surface edge, a published surface API narrower than its file set (mount + props + consumed projections only, never sub-components or private state shapes), imports terminating at the declaration (no deep imports past it), grouping by surface never by file kind with a folder-minting threshold (a surface earns a folder at its second private module — bounded scope, never size), shared tier as a strict leaf, a single IPC seam (one shared module performs core invocation), and total zone coverage. INV-10's lateral analogue: a surface's internals are unnameable by its siblings, as an adapter's representation is unnameable by the domain. Motivated by l2-crate-topology §6.4's real INV-2 violation — a module published more than it meant to and a downstream call site took what was offered. §4.4 resolves l2-quality-pipeline §4.1's preset-versus-custom-zones TBD in favour of custom zones, rejecting the bundled `feature-sliced` preset (its entities/features layers organize client-side domain logic that INV-2 forbids); enforcement mapped onto tripwire discipline TW-1/2/3/4/7/10/11 with the judgment part of UMT-2 declared a review obligation rather than faked as a check. §6 records full FSD adoption as considered and rejected, its three transferable rules adopted individually. | [l2-application-shell.md](specifications/l2-application-shell.md) | React 19 + Tauri v2 realization of the reactive frontend runtime AS-1…AS-13 — the L2 the application-shell model had never received, and the spec that settles a fork present in the source: `packages/ui` exports **two composition roots** (the shipped workbench the desktop app mounts, and a shell frame that is built, exported, tested and rendered by nothing). One root, with the earlier composer remounted as the office surface behind the router (R-1 one mount · R-2 the router owns placement · R-3 the root injects, no module-level singleton). Three state domains separated by *who owns the truth* — view (frontend), projection (core), session status — held in hand-rolled subscribable stores over a scoped external-store subscription, no state library added to a package that holds no domain state; a projection snapshot is *unrequested / pending / loaded / unavailable-with-reason*, never a default-empty a surface could mistake for a real empty result. The single seam gains an event direction (push, no polling) under a **capability-admission rule**: a bridge method may be added only when it binds a core capability already bound by another frontend (INV-3), so the seam cannot grow a frontend-only feature; unbacked projections stay explicit placeholders (INV-9). Namespaced actions with mandatory descriptive labels; keystrokes resolve against a focus-derived **context stack** with predicates, multi-key prefixes, most-specific-then-most-recently-layered precedence, and fall-through on unbound; bindings merge in three deterministic layers (base → platform → user) with origin shown. Workbench stated in the L1 vocabulary (workspace/center-panes/docks-panels/floating) with the shipped one-pane center declared a populated subset; versioned layout record persisted by the core (INV-5), restored field-wise so it can never block startup. Cancellation-owned async: an unmounted owner cancels, a late response is discarded, every rejection resolves to a visible state. States honestly where the stack lacks the L1 mechanism (AS-2 handles, AS-11 executors) and how the intent is met instead, rather than renaming primitives to make the table read uniformly ; v1.0.1 §4.3 admission rule reconciled to its own stated purpose — admissibility is *the bound capability exists in the core **or the host** and is not frontend-only*, with two named classes (a core capability another surface binds; a host-owned facility such as the settings-store-backed layout record / theming axes), CLI/TUI parity demoted from necessary condition to sufficient signal; repairs the contradiction with §4.5/§3 rather than adding a requirement  ; v1.1.0 §4.3's request/response direction collapses from **one method per bound capability** to **one generic dispatch** over the core's invocable catalog, plus a `catalog()` read and the unchanged push edge — the prior shape was the third hand-written statement of one input→core mapping, its TypeScript client was on course to be a fourth (F-1/F-7), and it is compile-time bound so it could never carry a contributed verb. The 1.0.1 admission rule is **preserved and given a machine-readable form**: its two admissible classes are the catalog's `locus` values (Semantic travels through dispatch; HostOnly keeps its own method and is *declared* as an exclusion), while the frontend-only case has no locus to declare and so cannot be registered — a review obligation becomes a structural one, with command-line parity still a sufficient signal and never a necessary condition. AS-6 clarified: the runtime's action registry is **not a second catalog** — it owns ClientLocal actions and sources Semantic ones from the core catalog by identity  ; v1.2.0 seam boundary — `catalog()` carries **descriptors only**, the executable face having no serialized form at all: not a rule the bridge applies per call but a property of the type (serializable outbound only), so no host handle can cross this seam and no inbound path can synthesize a descriptor the core did not author (SP-12); and this surface's catalog is an explicit **copy** for which `Unknown` is the staleness signal — dispatching an identity the registry no longer knows refreshes the catalog rather than rendering a failure, since a stale-copy problem reported generically reaches the user as a broken feature and the developer as an unreproducible bug (SP-13) | Stable | 2 | 1.2.0 |

Per-OS realization under one governing constraint — the background engine runs **as the user, never as a superuser** — which rejects the `LocalSystem` Windows Service and the root `LaunchDaemon` (both lose the per-user state root). Windows: `HKCU\…\Run` (login) / S4U scheduled task with At-startup trigger, `RunLevel=Limited` (system), reading `StartupApproved` veto alongside the Run value. macOS: `SMAppService.loginItem` / `LaunchDaemon` with `UserName`, mapping `requiresApproval` to a first-class state. Resolved at promotion: non-systemd Linux → login-scoped via XDG autostart + system-scoped `Unsupported` (BA-10); `tauri-plugin-autostart` rejected (std-first dependency policy); Windows S4U is design-settled, only At-startup launch-timing remains a build-time validation gate (with an `At log on` fallback). Divergence reconciled — `l2-technology-stack` amended to name the S4U task | Stable | 2 | 1.0.1 |

| [l1-input-binding.md](specifications/l1-input-binding.md) | Input binding — the **consumer-side twin** of `l1-output-contracts`, closing the asymmetry where nothing governed how a work unit obtains its inputs or what happens when it cannot (each surface parsed its own payload, invented its own failure shape, and discovered the problem after starting work). Declared ordered typed **binders** are the single source for both runtime supply and the advertised input schema, and the body may not reach into the raw envelope (IB-1). **Bind-before-invoke** makes *did not run* a structural outcome rather than a self-report, and the whole rejection class safely re-invocable (IB-2). A rejection is a typed first-class outcome on the normal channel with an effectively **uninhabited fault channel**, so a binding failure cannot be dropped by a forgetful intermediary (IB-3). Four distinguishable **located** modes — absent / unreadable / malformed / ill-shaped — because each implies a different corrective action *and a different responsible actor*, and the location turns a search into one edit (IB-4). **Optional means absent, never invalid** — a present-but-malformed value still rejects, closing the quiet-corruption path where a bad value becomes a default (IB-5). Two supply phases with **composition-phase needs sealed before mounting**, so an unmet dependency fails at assembly instead of on the first production call (IB-6); the untyped dynamic channel is a bounded fallback that never substitutes for a declarable need (IB-7). **Once-only inputs bind once and last, enforced at declaration** — two declared consumers is a construction error, not a runtime race (IB-8). Ordered short-circuit binding whose first rejection is stable and therefore learnable (IB-9); a **default ceiling on every caller-sized input**, the input-side twin of the ATE-12 output overflow guard (IB-10); composing/wrapping binders as the single-parse reuse mechanism (IB-11); and a **separate rejection channel**, since a rising rejection rate indicts the *advertisement*, not the implementation (IB-12). **nodus projection, no new construct**: move `@in` enforcement ahead of the first step (validation-class, like undefined-command/undefined-variable — recorded as a candidate amendment to the typed-I/O invariant, deliberately not applied), split the input error taxonomy into the four modes, and fix optional-input semantics onto the existing null value; ceiling and wrapping stay host-side. Composes output-contracts / agent-tool-ergonomics (ATE-2/ATE-12/ATE-13) / tool-call-transport (TCT-4, TCT-8) / tool-composition (TC-2) / interception-model (INT-10 boundary) / execution-graph (EG-2, EG-11) / declarative-configuration / security / completion-verification / practice-analytics (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |

| [l1-project-vocabulary.md](specifications/l1-project-vocabulary.md) | Project vocabulary — the governing artifact that lets a project say something **short and exact**, instead of re-describing a concept in twenty words every time it comes up. One canonical term per concept with its definition, because two live terms force every reader and producer to decide whether the difference carries meaning and they decide inconsistently (VOC-1); **displaced synonyms listed with the entry, never merely absent** — an absent synonym is invisible guidance, a listed one corrects *before* the wrong word is written (VOC-2, a negative specification at term granularity); **relationships** between terms, since confusion is almost always about connection rather than one word's meaning (VOC-3); ambiguities **resolved on the record**, so the same question is not re-litigated and older material stays decodable (VOC-4); the vocabulary governs **produced artifacts** — identifiers, module names, work-item titles, records — because one honoured in prose while the code says otherwise has *doubled* the terminology, and the code's version is what future readers believe (VOC-5); authored **during the work** when the distinction is live, since a vocabulary compiled afterwards captures what was already agreed and omits exactly the contested terms (VOC-6); a term **earns** canonical status through the codification pathway and a generating actor never self-promotes its own coinage (VOC-7); **authoritative, distinct from the client-facing glossary**, which is a derived plain-language projection — conflating them yields a governing artifact rewritten for readability and an outward document nobody may edit (VOC-8); precision the goal and volume a cost, an unconsulted vocabulary being **worse than none** since artifacts still claim to follow it (VOC-9); adherence and **compression** measured, the honest measure being that a settled term saves symbols on every occurrence forever while a term nobody uses saves nothing (VOC-10). **nodus projection, no new primitive**: the schema vocabulary is already a validated controlled vocabulary enforcing one name per operation (unknown command fails at validation), selective disclosure is already the volume control, and author-chosen names carrying a displaced synonym are lintable at the validate-before-run stage. | [l1-invariant-tripwires.md](specifications/l1-invariant-tripwires.md) | Invariant tripwires — the **enforcement half** the rule-producing layers never supplied: codification promotes a pattern to a ratified rule and specs state invariants, but neither asks *who notices when the rule is broken next time*, and the answer in practice is memory — the mechanism whose failure created the rule. A tripwire is a narrow automated check living with the code that fails when **one specific forbidden shape reappears**. A mechanically checkable rule carries a mechanical check (TW-1); the tripwire is authored **in the same change that earns the rule**, when the offending shape, the reason, and the alternative are all still precise and before they decay to folklore (TW-2); one tripwire per rule, named for the rule, so a failure reads as *this rule was violated* (TW-3); the failure names rule, site, reason, and sanctioned alternative — a check that teaches nothing gets suppressed rather than satisfied (TW-4); targeted and cheap, never a general style rule, since precision is the source of its authority (TW-5); **structural checks and behavioral tests are distinct kinds and both required** — a system can pass every behavior test while violating every structural rule, because the behavior is still correct *today* (TW-6); exemptions explicit, narrow, and reasoned **inside** the tripwire, never by widening the pattern (TW-7); a rule that stops holding is retired **with** its tripwire rather than quietly weakened, the worst state being a rule that looks enforced while matching nothing (TW-8); the enforcement **inventory is legible** — the uncovered set is the honest measure of how much governance runs on memory (TW-9); and the gate is on the **delta**, never accumulated state, since a tripwire failing on pre-existing debt is disabled and protects nothing (TW-10). **nodus projection, no new primitive**: the validate-before-run stage hosts authored-workflow tripwires, and the canonical-form round-trip guarantee gives them a **reformat-proof structural target** instead of a surface-text pattern. Composes pattern-codification (the producer) / convergence-gate (placement + delta) / quality-standards (a tier, not a parallel gate) / change-containment / completion-verification (CMP-2) / evaluation-suites (the behavioral sibling) / optimization-integrity / spec-driven-governance (tech-agnostic) ; v1.1.0 TW-11 a tripwire **declares what it cannot see** and records its precision trade — uncovered shapes named as known limits, accepted false positives recorded with why tightening costs more; prevents both broadening-until-noise (which gets the tripwire disabled) and the unstated gap that lets the TW-9 inventory count a rule as enforced while a class of violation walks past, a false assurance worse than no check; a declared limit is the honest input to TW-6 | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |

generation normalizes surface form across options and the property is **checked, not intended**, since a generator told to avoid the tell reproduces it | Evaluation suites — declarative, version-controlled test suites for agent customizations (skills/roles/workflows), the dynamic half of customization quality complementing the static document lint: suite-as-companion-artifact bound to its target (ES-1) with addressable golden tasks declaring inputs+expectations+graders (ES-2); typed grader taxonomy across five families — activation/invocation/behavior-budget/output-shape/judgment — composing existing validators, never redefining them (ES-3, reuses l1-output-contracts + behavior budget); global+task grader scopes (ES-4); weighted thresholded metrics with per-metric hard-gate, no opaque single score (ES-5); stability via repeated trials with variance reporting (ES-6); frozen+isolated-per-run, fixture-seeded, state-reset (ES-7); immutable attributed diffable results with full provenance (ES-8); baseline comparison + regression gate feeding skill evolution (ES-9); first-class activation-correctness testing — over/under-triggering as failures (ES-10); sandboxed no-production-side-effects reusing AP-13 §4.9 quarantine (ES-11); scaffold-then-maintain authoring, scaffold never authoritative coverage (ES-12); static-vs-dynamic two-halves framing; v1.1 control-arm attribution — credit only the margin over the best naive same-intent control, not baseline (ES-13); separated non-negotiable quality/safety tier with implicit-requirement adversarial tasks, no efficiency gain buys back a quality regression (ES-14); judge trust self-test + cite-the-construct auditability (ES-15); signed metrics never penalizing necessary additions as bloat (ES-16); sliceable results by orthogonal declared labels — per-slice macro-averaged reporting so a regression localizes to a capability/complexity/scenario/modality/source slice, not just the aggregate (ES-17), the suite-level companion to the l1-agent-coevaluation matrix; reference-free comparative judgment — a relative bias-guarded pairwise preference (A vs B) for open-ended tasks with no ground-truth reference, order/verbosity/self-enhancement guarded and non-transitivity-honest, a relative non-authoritative signal consumed by harness-optimization/iterative-refinement/competitive-execution and never an absolute metric (ES-18, §4.10); capability-dependency ordering — a task MAY depend on prerequisite tasks (a capability it builds on), evaluation topologically ordered, a dependent whose prerequisite failed reported blocked + attributed (by default not run, no budget on a foundation-broken capability), dependency on the prerequisite's verdict only never its state so ES-7 isolation preserved, a broken chain localized to its root (ES-19, §4.11); v1.5.0 ES-20 **dependency-selected, cost-tiered execution** — a slow or metered suite is not run in full on every change, since a check too expensive to run gets skipped and a skipped check is worse than an absent one (the suite still looks protective): tasks declare the artifacts whose change could alter their outcome (a designated global set selects everything; full runs stay on demand) and a declared tier — deterministic/fast/safety-relevant **gate** blocking every change vs non-deterministic/expensive/externally-dependent **periodic** reporting on a schedule; a partial run names what it skipped and yields an explicitly partial verdict, never a full-suite pass, and a safety-relevant task is never demoted to periodic to speed the gate (tech-agnostic). v1.7.0: ES-22 (in-house harness is corroborating, an external standard with an independent outcome check is decisive — report both; | [l1-facilitation.md](specifications/l1-facilitation.md) ; v1.3.0 FC-14/15/16 added - **stake tiering inside the frontier**: FC-13 decides which questions may be asked now (dependency), FC-14 decides how (consequence) - load-bearing questions asked **singly and in sequence** because each answer reshapes the rest, everything else issued as one **veto-by-exception** block, and every question carrying why-it-is-load-bearing + a committed recommendation + a **named consequence of guessing wrong**, the third being the **admission test** rather than decoration (a weak consequence line demotes the question), which is the mechanical fix for the low-value-questioning defect FC-13 names and deliberately leaves unnamed; **bulk acceptance is a granted delegation over a named set** - offered never assumed, naming the resolved set it covers at the moment it is given (CB-1/CB-2), and marking every decision it locks as **accepted-by-default** so a later reader can tell a deliberated choice from a defaulted one - absent all three it is a fabricated counterparty (XPL-4) in a shortcut's clothes, since silence is not the grant; **the resolved set presented once as a sourced batch** before questioning begins, each entry naming what settled it, and a **correction re-opening that entry as a question** along with everything downstream that rested on it ; v1.2.0 FC-13 added - **frontier-batched elicitation**: open questions form a dependency tree, a question depending on another still-open question belongs to a later round, and the agent asks the whole **frontier** in one round with a **recommended answer** attached to each, completing when the frontier empties rather than at a count; **facts are the agent's job, decisions the user's** (environment-discoverable facts looked up in parallel, blocking only their downstream questions); reconciles the apparent tension with FC-4 explicitly - FC-4 governs the *process* menu where a recommendation cannot substitute for the user's pick, while a content question carrying a recommendation is a proposal, not a wall; authoritative un-overridable managed tier set out-of-band (PG-2); surface lockdown — managed-only extensions/hooks/permission rules (PG-4); trusted extension-source restriction (PG-5); escape-hatch governance — disable safety-reducing modes like permission-bypass (PG-6);
