| [l1-generation-budget.md](specifications/l1-generation-budget.md) ; v1.1.0 GB-2 amended — the capped capacity is **not exclusively the visible answer's**: on a deliberating model the allowance is consumed **first** by generation the caller never receives, so truncation has **two causes with opposite remedies** — answer cut off mid-production (escalate, then GB-3 continue from the partial) vs allowance **exhausted before the answer began** (raise the cap or lower depth per GS-3; there is no partial, and running GB-3 here re-spends the whole budget on deliberation again) — mechanically separable by **zero delivered output**; the accounting for that class moves to `l1-reasoning-spend` RSN-2 | Output generation budget — the output-side member of the token economy (symmetric to context-compression on the input side): minimal default output reservation sized to the common case, not the worst case, so a request never over-reserves generation capacity/GPU-slot/cost (GB-1); detect length-truncation and escalate the output cap toward the model's real limit, never accepting a cut-off response as complete (GB-2); continue from the preserved partial across bounded turns, stitching the full result, never discarding (GB-3); bounded escalation+continuation so a runaway generation cannot loop (GB-4); decomposition fallback on exhaustion — force skeleton-then-fill instead of retrying the monolith (GB-5); truncation-safe artifacts — a partial write/edit is rejected, never half-applied (GB-6); distinct axis from input-context budgeting and from the output contract (GB-7); accounted/observable feeding operational-health (GB-8); composes with l1-context-compression / l2-context-management / l1-output-contracts / l1-model-runtime / l2-model-error-recovery (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |
| [l1-context-compression.md](specifications/l1-context-compression.md) | Context compression — the third token-economy stage beside selection (trim) and summarization (compaction): reversible/fidelity-bounded dense re-encoding of high-volume structured content (tool outputs, diffs, logs, dumps) to cut token cost while preserving usable information. Distinct from dropping/paraphrasing (CC-1); lossless-or-bounded-declared-loss (CC-2); recoverable original on demand (CC-3); content-aware eligibility — bulky structure yes, dense prose / actively-edited code no (CC-4); model legibility with expand-on-demand (CC-5); measured/accounted savings (CC-6); runs before eviction cascade (CC-7); composable, no double-compress corruption (CC-8); protected-region deny-list — caller-tagged/safety/crypto-thinking never compressed regardless of size, overrides CC-4 (CC-9); §4.5 compress-cache-retrieve realization of CC-3 (content-addressed retrieval handle + pluggable store + TTL, lossy-on-wire/lossless-end-to-end, retrieval as a normal tool call); live-zone-only per l1-cache-stable-context CSC-2; CC-10 memory-safe lossy reduction (§4.6) — the lossy stages (summarization/compaction + hard eviction) MUST be preceded by durable capture into the persistent memory store so a lossy reduction is loss-of-detail never loss-of-knowledge (user prefs/decisions/commitments/in-flight task state secured before the transcript carrying them is condensed); distinct from CC-3 (which keeps a compressed original recoverable by handle) — CC-10 protects the lossy stages where no inline original survives so the safety net is memory not a handle; satisfied by continuous per-turn capture while it keeps pace, backstopped by an ordered-before best-effort pre-reduction capture pass (failure non-fatal/retried, never blocks the turn); couples the token-economy family to l1-memory-intelligence/l1-memory-model so an indefinitely-running session sheds transcript bulk without losing what it learned (tech-agnostic); CC-11 content-type-routed signal-preserving reduction (§4.7) — the compression transform is selected by detecting the content's type or producing command (log/diff/JSON/code/markup/search or a recognized package-manager/container/test-runner command output) and each type's reducer keeps the high-value signal characteristic of that type (errors+traces in logs, hunk headers+changed lines in diffs, imports+signatures in code, schema+anomaly rows in JSON, readable text in markup, top matches+counts in search) while collapsing only the redundant remainder; deterministic-first — unstructured prose passes through untouched unless the host enables a semantic model-based reducer, blanket structure-blind truncation forbidden; sharpens CC-1 (type-specific not one blanket transform) + CC-4 (eligibility gains a how: detect→route→preserve-signal) [v1.4.0] CC-12 archive-backed recoverability — CC-3's recoverable original rests on a durable substrate (`l1-evidence-archive` EA-1/EA-2/EA-3) rather than expiring when content leaves the live context, and archive-before-reduce extends the discipline to the lossy stages CC-1 deliberately excludes. | Stable | 1 | 1.4.0 |
| [l1-cache-stable-context.md](specifications/l1-cache-stable-context.md) | Cache-stable context — prompt-cache stability discipline elevating scattered L2 precautions (l2-agent-registry prompt-cache parity, l2-session-checkpoint frozen blocks, l2-learning-loop cache isolation, l2-dashboard cache_read accounting) into one L1: provider cache key as a first-class cost lever (CSC-1); frozen-prefix/live-zone partition with a computed boundary — only the tail live zone is mutable, system+tools+prior turns frozen (CSC-2); append-only history (CSC-3); byte-faithful passthrough — no reserialization/key-reorder drift (CSC-4); deterministic live-zone edits, no timestamps/random (CSC-5); position preservation (CSC-6); sacrosanct crypto/thinking fields never altered (CSC-7); schema parity across parent↔fork (CSC-8); billing-mode-gated aggressiveness — metered aggressive, subscription/session conservative-passthrough never voiding scope (CSC-9); observe-and-confine never rewrite-to-align anti-pattern (CSC-10); separated cache_read/cache_creation/input/output accounting so a cache bust is detectable from telemetry not the invoice (CSC-11); orthogonal to context-compression (what to shrink) — this governs where mutation is allowed at all (tech-agnostic); CSC-12 the boundary is a stable seam not a byte offset — the partition point CSC-2 computes is a position the encoder declares stable (one no continuation can move), not an arbitrary offset into the bytes, because encoding is not composable (l1-tokenization-boundary TB-2) and content appended in the live zone can retroactively re-encode the tail of the frozen prefix while leaving every one of its bytes untouched (key byte-identical, symbol sequence changed); CSC-4 byte-faithful passthrough and CSC-6 position preservation are therefore necessary but NOT sufficient conditions for a hit; anchoring the boundary on a control symbol (a message/turn frame marker) satisfies the rule by construction (TB-4), which is why the natural partition falls between whole messages and never mid-message, and a boundary whose stability the encoder does not assert is unstable (TB-3); the storage-side statement of the same rule is l1-inference-cache IC-10 | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |
| [l1-context-degradation.md](specifications/l1-context-degradation.md) | Context degradation & health — the failure-model the context-economy family exists to prevent, and the discipline that keeps a long context healthy not merely small: degradation is a **continuum managed on a risk signal, not the token limit** — acting only at the hard limit means already operating degraded (CD-1); **positional salience** — attention is positionally non-uniform (strong head/tail, weak middle), so load-bearing content (task, hard requirements, findings-to-act-on) goes in the high-attention head/tail zones never buried mid-context, placement a deliberate assembly decision composing the cache-stable frozen-prefix(head)/live-zone(tail) (CD-2); **non-adversarial poisoning** as a distinct failure from adversarial injection — a wrong/stale/hallucinated fragment entering on an honest path (bad tool result, stale doc, hallucinated summary) contaminates downstream reasoning and persists despite correction, defense is reasoning-hygiene not a boundary check (CD-3, complement of context-provenance CP); **recovery is excision to a verified state, not correction-in-place** — appending "ignore that" leaves poisoned references and the contamination compounds; only verified content carried forward, durable knowledge preserved via CC-10 (CD-4); behavioral not size-only detection (quality drop on previously-successful work, tool misalignment, persistent error, contradictory outputs) feeding operational-health, kin to OI-1 (CD-5); taxonomy-matched mitigation write/select/compress/isolate/excise/reconcile never one blunt reduction — volume-reduction on a poisoning/clash problem treats the symptom (CD-6); working-context clash surfaced+reconciled to one basis never silently averaged, the MI-4 counterpart (CD-7); owns the degradation phenomenology l1-context-compression/l2-context-management/l1-progressive-disclosure/ORC-5 each answer without a unifying model; content-side sibling of l1-optimization-integrity; nodus-relevance needs no new NL invariant — host-side salience over NL-15 + re-run-from-verified-checkpoint over the execution-graph/pinned-partial seam (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-optimization-integrity.md](specifications/l1-optimization-integrity.md) | Optimization integrity — the cross-cutting verification discipline for **silently-degradable optimizations** (context compression, cache-prefix stability, output shaping, ranked recall, inference-cache warmth) whose breach produces no error and well-formed output, degrading only cost/latency/answer-quality (a busted cache returns correct answers at 10× the bill — nothing throws, no functional test fails): named silent-failure class with mandatory integrity coverage, never relying on exceptions/schema/functional tests a silent breach doesn't trip (OI-1); runtime violation sensor for byte-exact/emergent contracts — a must-stay-zero counter alarmed on first live divergence (e.g. bytes-modified on a promised byte-faithful passthrough path), because a bust emergent from live per-instance state cannot be staged by a static test (OI-2); correctness parity measured-not-asserted via version-controlled fixture (with vs without) or held-out control (OI-3); efficacy parity — per-stage saving, counterfactual reported as estimate+confidence-range or held-out measured number, never a fabricated exact figure (OI-4); tail-aware distribution parity/regression gate — p50/p95/p99 AND mean, failing if ANY regresses, so a tail-only regression can't hide behind the median when a change/impl-swap is admitted (OI-5, generalizes retrieval-evaluation RE-6); bounded-cardinality code-closed observation dimensions — every label a closed code-fixed vocabulary, untrusted/unbounded input mapped to `other` and surfaced, never a raw dimension (OI-6, cost/DoS + forgery hardening composing LL-6/CP-1); verify-don't-repair-or-hide measure-and-surface boundary feeding operational-health, remediation to l1-doctor (OI-7); fail-visible — an unrun check is `unverified`, never rendered as `verified` (OI-8); unifies scattered per-surface guards CSC-4/CSC-11, IC-7, RE-6, GS-6 into one contract; nodus-relevance needs no new NL invariant (host-side AuditProvider HO-8/HO-9 + `!!` + RUN(@macro) reuse gating) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-artifact-derived-observation.md](specifications/l1-artifact-derived-observation.md) ; v1.1.0 ADO-12/13/14 — a figure describing a bounded period expires when **that period rolls over**, not on a clock, with unreadable window metadata marking validity unknown rather than discarding a real measurement, and a re-measurement interval defending against repeated opens but never against a person explicitly asking (ADO-12); a source with no per-event identity is admitted as a **lower bound**, never summed and never discarded, overlapping sources merging identified event *sets* not counts (ADO-13); an authoritative provider figure sits **beside** the derived one with its own provenance and freshness, never absorbing or being back-filled from it, its credential travelling nowhere but that request (ADO-14) | Artifact-derived observation — the **uninstrumented-source** sibling of telemetry: how the office learns about a tool it does not control (an external agent, a coding tool the human runs beside it, a build system) by reading the durable trail that tool already writes for its own purposes. **Out-of-band by construction** — never wrap, proxy, shim, or inject, and never modify what is read; the guarantee bought is that the observer's failure cannot become the observed tool's failure, and it is void the moment anything enters that path (ADO-1). The artifact's shape is **discovered, never promised**: each reader declares what it expects and validates first, so a mismatch is a typed per-source coverage gap, never a run-ending error and never a silent reinterpretation yielding plausible numbers from an unrecognized structure (ADO-2). **Absent / unreadable / empty are three different answers** — collapsing them produces the characteristic failure of the whole approach, a confident zero read as a fact about the world; only the third is a real zero (ADO-3). One independently-failing adapter per source (ADO-4); discovery **declared, never a disk sweep** that reads what nobody agreed to expose and makes the observer's footprint unauditable (ADO-5); adapters loaded only on evidence their tool exists, so supporting N tools costs nothing to the user who has one (ADO-6). **One underlying event counted once** across overlapping sources via a derived event identity, with an unresolvable case **disclosed** rather than quietly inflated or quietly deleted — inflation is visible and correctable, deletion is neither (ADO-7). Incremental by cheap fingerprint with a versioned **interpretation**, so a corrected reader cannot leave stale conclusions standing behind an unchanged input (ADO-8). **Foreign artifact text is untrusted input** — a transcript another agent wrote can carry an injected instruction, and a file on the user's own disk is a location, not a provenance (ADO-9). Derived never authoritative, on-device, secret-safe (ADO-10). **Coverage travels with every number** — a figure from partial coverage presented as a total is this approach's defining dishonesty (ADO-11). **nodus projection, no new primitive**: reading is a declared read-only host effect, and existing value-provenance + origin-taint labels already carry *derived* and *untrusted* independently. Composes telemetry (boundary) / interception-model (the in-path alternative declined for foreign tools) / context-provenance / practice-analytics (PA-6/PA-7) / progressive-disclosure / agent-federation (the cooperating-peer complement) / security (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |
| [l1-outcome-attributed-cost.md](specifications/l1-outcome-attributed-cost.md) | Outcome-attributed cost — the closing half of the cost family, which measured **outflow** (meter → rate → allowance → enforce) and could not distinguish an expensive session that shipped from an equally expensive one reverted the next morning. Every unit of accounted spend is attributed to the durable output it produced **or explicitly to none**, with no third implicit state and a large unattributed share reported as *the finding* rather than excluded from the denominator (OAC-1). **Survival is a later, separately-observed fact** over a declared window, never inferred at production time — *unresolved* is an honest state, and marking work "shipped" when it is produced records an intention as an outcome (OAC-2). A **closed outcome vocabulary** — kept / superseded / reverted / abandoned / **discarded-by-design** / unresolved — whose load-bearing separation is that a deliberate best-of-N's losing branches (CE-*) are the declared price of a strategy, not a failure: fuse them with *reverted* and an office running competitive execution reads its own working mechanism as its largest waste (OAC-3). **Spend with no durable output is a measured category carrying no implicit verdict**, since the same number means opposite things for exploration and for a tenth attempt at one bug (OAC-4). Attribution **reconstructed from records already kept** (ledger, work item, change history, receipts) — a parallel hand-maintained mapping is forbidden because a drifted attribution still produces confident numbers (OAC-5); shared/uncertain attribution expressed, never resolved by an undeclared tiebreak (OAC-6); per-unit figures reported with denominator, dispersion, and minimum-sample abstention (OAC-7); **never a verdict on a person** — structurally absent, not filtered at presentation, because a measure that scores people is gamed and a gamed measure still carries a number's authority (OAC-8); **retrospective, never an in-flight gate**, since wiring survival statistics into live selection closes a loop in which the measure shapes what it measures (OAC-9); counterfactual honesty, no fabricated savings (OAC-10). **nodus projection, no new primitive**: the run record carries the cost half and gains a declared outcome slot; competitive selection labels its own discards at the source; a later survival verdict arrives through the existing correlated-later-completion path. Composes cost-rating / usage-allowance (UA-8) / competitive-execution / version-control (VC-3 join) / operational-ledger / practice-analytics (PA-15 gains a money dimension) / artifact-derived-observation / user-model (UM-8) — distinct from change-attribution (statistical regression blame) and value-settlement (outbound payment) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

unavailable counts make the class **unmeasured** and containing aggregates **declared lower bounds**, the count-side twin of CR-4's fail-visible unknown *rate* | Cost rating & pricing — the owned middle step of the cost pipeline (**meter → rate → charge**) that turns a metered usage record into an honest monetary figure, distinct from metering (unit counts) and charging (budget enforcement): single-producer derivation so its honesty rules live in one place (CR-1); per-class rates never one blended rate — fresh input/output/cache_read/cache_creation/modality units each at their own rate, composing CSC-11 + HO-8 class separation (a cache-read unit bills at a fraction of fresh input) (CR-2); honest rate resolution — exact then family/base with dated-suffix normalization, an inferred rate marked never passed as exact, never a different model's rate substituted (CR-3); **fail-visible unknown rate — an unpriced model is priced `UNKNOWN`, never a silent $0** that under-reports spend and corrupts every aggregate (the cost-side of OI-8), aggregates including unknowns disclosed as lower bounds (CR-4); explicit currency with immutable native amount, conversion an explicit dated provenance-marked transform never a hardcoded factor (CR-7); reconstructable/auditable priced record (resolved rate+provenance+basis, per-class counts, per-class+total cost) so mispricing is detectable from the record not the invoice, feeding OH-6 + OI-4 (CR-8); owns the `catalog pricing` MB-3/l2-dashboard/l2-model-router/l2-deep-research assumed without a contract; consumers = l2-budget-engine (enforce) / l1-usage-allowance (windows) / l1-routing (cost signal) / l1-dashboard (display); | [l1-adjustment-vocabulary.md](specifications/l1-adjustment-vocabulary.md) | Adjustment vocabulary — the client's cheap, named handle on a produced artifact's own dimensions, replacing free-form revision (expensive at both ends, ambiguous in both directions, and reliably answered with a regeneration that discards accumulated agreement). An adjustment **edits** the existing artifact, never regenerates it — the single most expensive failure the concept prevents (ADJ-1); the vocabulary is **closed, named, and shared**, since re-interpretation per turn is free-form revision with ceremony (ADJ-2); every axis is **bidirectional**, because a one-way verb is a ratchet whose only remedy for overshoot is the rewrite ADJ-1 forbids (ADJ-3); one axis per adjustment, or the move is unattributable (ADJ-4); re-applicable and bounded, with the producer **saying so at the limit** rather than moving something else to appear responsive (ADJ-5); reversible along the axis — an "opposite" yielding a third unrelated result is no axis at all (ADJ-6); axes declared **per artifact kind**, since a universal set collapses into *more*/*less* (ADJ-7); an adjustment carries **no verdict** on the previous version, so steering is not read as failure and answered with defence or over-correction (ADJ-8); free-form direction always available with a promotion path into the vocabulary (ADJ-9); the trail recorded as axis+direction+version — replayable, truncatable, and readable as **revealed preference** feeding the user model (ADJ-10). **nodus projection, no new primitive**: an adjustment is a revise step with a declared axis, the human-in-the-loop dialog channel carries it, the memoizable-decision path turns a repeated steer into a standing preference, and per-step trace annotations hold the trail. Distinct from iterative-refinement (automated grader loop, frozen rubric) and from directability (steers office substrate, not artifact dimensions); composes generation-shaping / design-identity (craft floor) / conversational-control / pattern-codification / user-model / conversation-rewind (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-negative-specification.md](specifications/l1-negative-specification.md) ; v1.1.0 NEG-11 added - the **positive target is the primary form** an exclusion takes when it reaches a generator: steering by prohibition places the excluded behaviour into the generator's context where it becomes *more* available, the negation being a weak modifier over a strongly activated concept, so NEG-5's name-the-alternative is hardened into an ordering (render the exclusion as its positive target, prohibition as justification) with the bare ban reserved for hard guardrails no positive form can carry; the checker side NEG-9 is explicitly unaffected, since detection and steering have opposite requirements and only steering pays the availability cost | Negative specification — the named shared shape behind four locally-invented instances (a corrected assumption promoted to an anti-pattern, generic-default tells blocked at a craft bar, a forbidden construct under a source tripwire, a banned register in generated prose): a declared, provenanced, bounded statement of what an output must **not** be. An exclusion is a first-class artifact with identity, rationale, origin, and an affirmation date — not a footnote or a conversation (NEG-1); **positive and negative are independent and neither implies the other**, so a system modelling only the target passes every check while producing exactly what the principal asked to avoid (NEG-2); three origins with different conflict authority — principal-declared / derived / built-in (NEG-3); the exclusion **reaches the generator before production**, review enforcement being the second line and never the only one, since a negative caught after the fact costs a rewrite while the same constraint in context costs tokens (NEG-4); the **alternative is named** wherever one exists, because a bare prohibition moves the generator to the next-most-default option (NEG-5); bounded, dated, revisited — an unbounded avoid-list is unsatisfiable and then ignored wholesale (NEG-6); an explicit principal request **outranks** an exclusion with the conflict surfaced once, an exclusion being no veto over its author (NEG-7); a violation names the matched signal and its origin, not an opinion in a finding's clothes (NEG-8); the checkable subset checked and the taste remainder named advisory, boundary stated (NEG-9); firing rate measured, where never-fires and always-fires are both actionable, the latter indicting the *positive* spec (NEG-10). **nodus projection, no new primitive**: the validated `§config` surface carries the set, the validation stage hosts the checkable subset, cache-stable composition places it in the byte-stable prefix, and an externally-sourced avoid-list carries provenance like any other content. Composes specialty-exemplars (the positive counterpart) / design-identity (DI-5/DI-6/DI-7, the worked instance) / invariant-tripwires (the enforcement mechanism for the checkable subset) / intent-resolution (IR-7 derived origin) / pattern-codification / content-language / user-model / cache-stable-context (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |

the latter is repaired by a **bounded automated re-anchoring** — the producing actor is handed the *precise* mismatch (which unit, recorded base, actual current content) and re-emits *just the failed delta* re-anchored against truth under a declared retry budget (composing output-contracts OC-4/OC-5), the terminal stale-base blocker (CM-9) deferred until the budget exhausts not raised on first failure; | [l1-order-independent-production.md](specifications/l1-order-independent-production.md) | Order-independent production — the property that makes splitting a multi-unit artifact **safe**, assumed by the staffing and decomposition specs and never stated: the unit at position *i* is a **pure function of *i* and a frozen input set, and of nothing else** (OIP-1), from which arbitrary concurrency, resumption, partial repair, and machine-independent results all follow. Four named prohibitions inside a producer: production order / neighbour residue; **wall-clock** (position is the clock); **unseeded randomness** — deliberate variation draws from a **position-derived seed**, stable per unit and still different across units; and live sources not pinned into the frozen set (OIP-2). One frozen input identity shared identically, since a differing parameter yields a *different artifact* assembled from parts of two (OIP-3); every producer receives its **global offset**, not merely a local extent, or it produces wrong units and reports success — invisible until assembly (OIP-4); uniform partitions with the remainder last, a **production** constraint because partition size decides where seams land (OIP-5); a declared, ordered, **seam-safe** join, seam-safety being a property of how parts were produced and unrecoverable at assembly (OIP-6). **The characteristic failure is cross-unit inconsistency, not a crash** — each unit individually valid, so a per-unit gate passes a thoroughly broken artifact and detection must be cross-unit: boundary continuity, monotonicity of running quantities, shared-fact agreement, and re-production equality (the cheapest, catching all four prohibitions) (OIP-7). **Reducing concurrency is a diagnosis, never a remedy** — serial production supplies the very ordering the units illegitimately relied on, so shipping width=1 leaves the defect, forfeits the capabilities, and stays machine-dependent while looking correct on the machine that made it (OIP-8). Asynchronous readiness is a **named, bounded** hold on one unit — the name making a timeout attributable to the work that stalled, the bound preventing a parallel run from silently becoming a hang (OIP-9); any subset independently re-producible, the basis for partial repair and resumption (OIP-10). **nodus projection, no new primitive**: a positional loop is the carrier (its loop variable *is* the position), the deferred-step mechanism is the named hold, and three of the four prohibitions are statically flaggable at the validate-before-run stage. Distinct from competitive-execution (alternatives where one survives) and change-merge (overlapping edits needing conflict resolution); composes parallel-staffing / incremental-execution (IE-4) / recursive-decomposition (RD-7) / execution-graph (EG-12) / reproduction-recipe (RR-8) / invariant-tripwires (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-parallel-staffing.md](specifications/l1-parallel-staffing.md) ; v1.1.0 PS-10 added - an ephemeral instance's **execution tier** (model class, reasoning depth, effort) is assigned from what **its own partition** demands and stated alongside the width, **never inherited from the coordinator**: inheritance is the default fan-out makes expensive, since the coordinator's tier is set for the hardest thing *it* does, fan-out multiplies that per-unit rate by the width, and the widest fans are characteristically the **shallowest** work (gathering, scanning, summarizing) where the coordinator's tier buys nothing; symmetric and neither direction a saving - a partition genuinely needing the higher tier gets it, and flattening the whole fan to the cheapest tier is a quality decision disguised as an economy - what is forbidden is the tier following **where an instance was spawned from** rather than what it was spawned to do; PS-2 disjointness is the ownership precondition of a launch episode | Parallel staffing — same-specialty scale-out: a large task MAY be worked by several parallel workers of ONE specialty (subagent instances of one role), the horizontal complement to ORC-2 vertical topology growth and l1-deliberation perspective fan-out: volume-justified recorded scale-out decision, one worker is the default never silent width (PS-1); parallelism ONLY through disjoint decomposition — never two workers on one unit, task-graph partitions claimed exclusively (WL-1) dispatched exactly-once (WL-9), non-decomposable serial residue stays serial — no promised speed-up it cannot deliver (PS-2); isolated siblings, mediated observable coordination (ORC-5/ORC-8/ORC-12), shared artifacts via change-merge never blind co-editing (PS-5); first-class fan-in — lead integrates, reviewable merge (CM), completion = integrated whole passes quality gates not last-partition-done (PS-6); learning consolidates to the single hired employee's memory scope attributed per instance — no knowledge fragmentation across throwaway identities (PS-7); failure containment per delegation boundary (ORC-11), stranded partition reaped-then-reassigned (WL-5-consistent), partial output salvaged only via merge (PS-8); | [l1-employee-availability.md](specifications/l1-employee-availability.md) | Employee availability states — the workforce-level state model between ROL-3 hire and ROL-4 fire, the office metaphor's honest rendering of runtime facts: closed exclusive taxonomy available / working / resting (short resource-driven day-off) / on-leave (planned vacation) / truant (detected anomaly), released = terminal lifecycle exit with re-hire → available, extension = versioned amendment (EMP-1); office state dominates — Paused/Hibernating suspends everyone uniformly, employee states are staffing/resource facts within a running office never a worker-side pause override (EMP-2, OC); truancy detected never declared — assigned + silent + no declared cause via the WL-8 watchdog, no setter command exists, surfaces + opens WL-6/doctor recovery, reclassifies on evidence, never resolves silently (EMP-3); rest is resource-honest with automatic return — declared cause only (model quota/rate/cost/backoff after OC-3 substitution attempted), per-employee OC-4 analog, the employee-level pressure valve preceding office hibernation (EMP-4); leave is planned manager-owned (ROL-5) with drain-or-hand-off first — no unit stays owned by a vacationer (WL-3 preserved), auto-return at window end or recall (EMP-5); no delegation to the unavailable — orchestrator substitutes/staffs (OFF-4/WSL-6) or queues with visible reason; delegation only — status queries and conversation remain served (EMP-6); visible evented causal — state+cause+expected return in the live projection, event before transition completes, auditable history (EMP-7, OC-5 discipline); availability never destroys knowledge — memory/skills/hierarchy untouched, only release archives and non-destructively (EMP-8); honest metaphor binding — mechanical truth always exposed alongside the metaphor, a lens never a curtain (EMP-9) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-project-priority.md](specifications/l1-project-priority.md) | Project priority — cross-office resource-arbitration signal: an explicit user/board-set ordered priority per office/project governing how a finite resource shared across offices (a building-level token/budget pool, or an external provider rate/quota all offices call through) is split when it becomes scarce and more than one office contends — the economic layer neither budget-engine (per-scope ceilings, hard-stop, no redistribution by design) nor global-orchestration (coordination/routing, sees budget but never ranks offices) supplies: explicit ordered priority per office, declared never inferred, the single arbitration key (PP-1); inert under abundance — every active office runs unconstrained regardless of rank, active ONLY under contention (PP-2); scarcity an explicitly detected evented condition — shared pool nearing cap / provider rate-limit / global ceiling pressure, never a guess, clears when the resource recovers (PP-3); priority-ordered/weighted allocation of the scarce remainder, declared strict-vs-weighted policy never a hidden heuristic, higher rank never served worse (PP-4); fairness floor — bounded starvation via floor/aging, low-priority slowed not silently zeroed, hard-preempt only as explicit opt-in surfaced with cause (PP-5); graceful yield — degrade NEW grants first, running units finish or checkpoint (CR-5), never a violent mid-op kill (GO-2), priority change affects future allocation only never retroactive clawback (PP-6); single building-level arbitration point at the global orchestrator that alone sees all offices' budgets/states (GO-1/GO-4), offices never negotiate the pool among themselves, no global orchestrator = no arbitration (PP-7); | [l1-project-support.md](specifications/l1-project-support.md) | Project support — the office's operational posture for a delivered/live project, the ongoing upkeep that begins where the build ends and neither development-workflow (per-change build pipeline) nor issue-reporting (user telling Cronus's makers about Cronus itself) models: a distinct declared build/support/mixed posture entered after first delivery, per project and per component, declared never inferred (SUP-1); reactive multi-source intake — inbound issues/bug reports, content-staleness signals, user/client requests, dependency & security upkeep, office-surfaced improvements — each captured as a work item with source recorded, never lost (SUP-2); triage-before-work by category/severity/priority reusing the office's existing inbound triage, no parallel intake (SUP-3); continuous with NO terminal done, right-sized to a steady maintenance load by adaptive staffing WSL-6 (SUP-4); every support change through the SAME mandatory quality gates as build work, regression the defining risk, no small-change bypass (SUP-5); closed typed category taxonomy corrective/content/perfective/preventive-adaptive each with its own priority driver (SUP-6); severity-driven expedite/hotfix path that changes urgency only never correctness discipline — still passes gates and authority boundaries (SUP-7); end-to-end issue→change→verification→delivery traceability, resolved/deferred/rejected explicitly never silently closed (SUP-8); same human-in-the-loop authority/budget/security/egress boundaries as any work (SUP-9); honest observable status — open items by category/severity, ages, deferral reasons surfaced not buried, a lens never a curtain (SUP-10); each item flows through the existing development pipeline + gates, support supplies the reactive front and honest back; demarcated from development-workflow/issue-reporting/workspace-lifecycle/kanban-model/project-priority in §4.5 (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-competitive-execution.md](specifications/l1-competitive-execution.md) ; v1.2.0 CE-11 added - **design-space** contests: N independent samples of one brief converge, so CE-7's budget buys near-duplicates, and such a contest **assigns each attempt a different declared constraint** (minimize the interface, maximize extensibility, optimize the common caller, design around the seam) so variants are *made* to differ, comparing on **axes declared before the attempts start**; the second half is a **boundary rather than an exception** - a design exploration's output is a *decision* not an artifact to integrate, so CE-4/CE-5 stand unchanged for artifact tournaments and a design contest hands its candidates to the **deliberation** protocol, where a reasoned blend is legitimate and recorded with named provenance | Best-of-N quality-selection fan-out — the third coordination-family mode beside parallel-staffing (throughput/integrate-all) and deliberation (synthesis/blend-all): when a work unit's outcome quality is genuinely uncertain and budget allows, the office runs N independent **complete** attempts at the **same whole unit**, each in an isolated context / scratch worktree (CE-2/CE-3, deliberately inverting parallel-staffing PS-2's disjoint-partition rule — made safe because attempts never co-write mainline and only one survives), an **independent judge** selects exactly one whole winner by a declared criterion (gate-objective / scored / judged, CE-4 — not a vote, not a blend), the **losing attempts are discarded never merged** (CE-5 — salvaging a loser's idea is a separate recorded act), and the winner is **integrated + gated as one whole** (CE-6 — a judge pick is a ranking, not completion); uncertainty-justified & recorded (CE-1), bounded width & honest afford / no nested tournaments (CE-7), contained failure & honest all-fail (CE-8), observable & attributed contest incl. the discard (CE-9); composes ORC-5/ORC-6/ORC-7/ORC-11/ORC-12 + CM + quality-gates + WL claim/reap; nodus realization = l1-nodus-language NL-13 competitive `~PARALLEL` `+select`/`~PICK`; v1.1.0 CE-10 **proxy-fidelity selection** — the N attempts MAY run at a reduced, *declared* fidelity used only to select, the winner re-produced at full fidelity before entering mainline (CE-6), changing the tournament's economics from N full attempts to N cheap plus one full and making width affordable where CE-7's budget headroom would otherwise cap N at one; three binding conditions each closing a way the saving becomes a lie — **uniform fidelity across attempts** (a contest of unequally-resourced candidates measures fidelity rather than quality and crowns whichever tried hardest), **the proxy validated not assumed** (a low-fidelity ranking is a hypothesis of agreement with the full-fidelity ranking, measured periodically against full-fidelity contests — a proxy that does not rank alike is worse than no tournament since it selects wrongly with confidence and pays to do so), and **the proxy recorded** in the result (CE-9) and in the winner's reproduction recipe so the shipped artifact is never confused with the sketch that won; on unvalidatable or degraded agreement the contest reverts to full fidelity and reports that it did (tech-agnostic); v1.1.1 links `l1-fidelity-variants` as the general axis CE-10 is the worked special case of — proxy attempts are declared variants, the winner's re-production is a move to the reference variant | Stable | 1 | 1.2.0 |

reuse requires a byte-stable prefix — the storage-side counterpart to l1-cache-stable-context authoring, the cache rewards stability never forces it (IC-9); §4.3 two-residency-budgets (model weights MR-6 vs cached state) coordinated on one memory so caching never starves model residency; composes l1-cache-stable-context / l1-model-runtime / l1-storage-model / l1-file-management / l1-security; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-language NL-15 (cache-stable prompt composition, byte-stable reusable prefix); IC-10 a shared prefix is a *symbol* prefix cut at a stable seam — IC-9's byte-stability is necessary but not sufficient, because encoding is not composable (l1-tokenization-boundary TB-2): a byte-identical leading context is a symbol prefix of the full request only when its boundary falls on a declared stable seam a continuation cannot move; a prefix whose final content can merge across the cut with the divergent tail has a stable key and an unstable sequence, so the cache either misses forever (a warmth regression IC-7 surfaces as a mystery) or serves state computed for a symbol sequence the request does not have — a silent IC-2 violation no key comparison can detect; prefix keys are therefore anchored at stable seams (a control symbol is one by construction, TB-4), an unknown boundary is treated as unstable, and seam stability is asserted by the encoder (TB-3) never inferred from the bytes | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |

| [l1-progressive-disclosure.md](specifications/l1-progressive-disclosure.md) | Progressive disclosure (tiered on-demand context loading) — the lazy-loading member of the context-economy family, orthogonal to compression (defer-loading vs shrink-loaded): every disclosable artifact (skill/tool/knowledge/schema) keeps only a minimal always-resident descriptor in context (PD-1), expands its body on trigger never preemptively (PD-2), reaches deeper bundled resources lazily level-by-level (PD-3); the descriptor is a routing contract whose accuracy is a correctness property — over-claim causes mis-trigger rot, under-claim causes missed use (PD-4); selective loading decouples catalog breadth from resident cost and bounds context rot (PD-5); deferral is lossless + reversible with collapse-back to the descriptor (PD-6); tiers are addressable/stable and the descriptor index sits in the frozen prefix so it stays cache-stable (PD-7); honest expanded-vs-deferred state so availability ≠ loaded (PD-8); composes l2-skill-system (the canonical 3-tier artifact) / l1-context-compression (the shrink-loaded axis) / l1-cache-stable-context (frozen descriptor index) / l1-harness-composition / l1-tool-composition; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-language NL-16 (`@needs` selective vocabulary disclosure) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

| [l1-agent-federation.md](specifications/l1-agent-federation.md) | Agent federation — interoperation with independent external agents as verifiable peers (the missing counterparty class beside human client / internal roles / SaaS connectors), deliberately crypto-agnostic (owns the trust contract, defers scheme/transport/settlement to L2/host): sovereign self-held credential that is simultaneously identity + authenticator + authorization root, no third party holds the secret, the credential IS the identity (AF-1); human-rooted never self-minted, issuance/rotation/revocation are principal acts composing SEC-10 (AF-2); signed capability advertisement (agent card) + open capability-based discovery, the card a minimal descriptor PD-1 (AF-3); authenticated-confidential E2E peer channels bound to both identities (AF-4); coordinate-without-controlling relay neutrality — routes ciphertext + settles, holds no plaintext/keys, cannot forge/read/censor (AF-5); default-deny peer trust — discoverability grants nothing, peer output untrusted (provenance), reaching a peer is an egress act (AF-6); accountable verifiable append-only exchange anchored to a both-sides-checkable proof, optional shielding + escrow, an event-log-plus-verifier not an asserted balance (AF-7); auditable attribution to the principal, public activity views are projections not the record (AF-8); §4.3 demarcated from l1-orchestration / l1-global-orchestration (own agents, shared authority) and l1-acp (a caller to one office); composes l1-security / l1-attestation / l1-tool-receipts / l1-context-provenance / l1-operational-ledger / l1-extension-marketplace; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-portability LP-14 (verified-peer delegation seam) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

| [l1-deployment-neutrality.md](specifications/l1-deployment-neutrality.md) | Deployment neutrality — local-first server-free default as the whole product, with a pluggable door open for auth + remote user-data backend: single local principal, no account/sign-in/remote-service/egress out of the box (DN-1); principal identity, authentication, and user-data persistence/sync are abstract provider seams with on-device defaults, the core assuming no server (DN-2); a remote backend is additive and opt-in — the *same core* runs solo-local, self-hosted sync server, or multi-tenant provider platform by supplying seam implementations only, without editing the core (DN-3); remote persistence and egress are consent-gated and never silent, covert off-device persistence unrepresentable (DN-4); a backend provider is an infrastructure-class extension (l1-extensions lifecycle, default-deny, manifest, sandbox, attestation, vetting), one active provider per plane, backing a core plane rather than adding a capability (DN-5); multi-tenancy is an optional hosted overlay not a core assumption, solo installs pay no tenancy tax (DN-6); the principal's data is portable across backends with the local default always a valid destination — no lock-in (DN-7); a backend moves data + authentication, never the agent's right to act — authority still descends from the human principal (DN-8, composes SEC-10 / AF-2); composes l1-architecture (INV-8) / l1-security / l1-extensions / l1-storage-model / l2-multi-user-auth / l1-multi-device-sync / l1-agent-federation; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-portability LP-15 (host-supplied durable-state seam, StorageProvider concretized) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

| [l1-data-lineage.md](specifications/l1-data-lineage.md) | Data lineage — derivation traceability across a dataflow, answering "what produced this datum, through which transformations", the fourth provenance-family contract distinct from trust (context-provenance), integrity (attestation), and grounding (claim-verification): item-grained dataflow with per-item mapping as the traceability granularity (LN-1); every derived item records its immediate source at production time, runtime-captured not inferred (LN-2); transitive end-to-end traceability by walking the links (LN-3); faithful links neither fabricated nor lost, recording true fan-out/merge/filter/aggregate correspondences (LN-4); side-band metadata that never alters payload or logic, stripping it yields identical business results (LN-5); composes the operational ledger + grounding at the data grain, distinct from trust/integrity/claim contracts (LN-6); survives suspension/resume and sub-flow boundaries (LN-7); references-not-copies, retention/consent-bounded, never a covert second copy of user data (LN-8); composes l1-automation-pipeline / l1-execution-graph / l1-operational-ledger / l1-claim-verification; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-observability HO-13 (per-item derivation lineage as optional side-band event metadata) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.1 |

| [l1-pattern-codification.md](specifications/l1-pattern-codification.md) ; v1.1.0 PC-4 clarified and the **missing axis named** — the pathway measured recurrence across **time** only, so observations repeated on distinct dates inside **one** context cleared every gate and produced a ratified rule silently claiming to hold everywhere; time-recurrence now explicitly establishes the pattern is **real**, never that it **generalizes**, with applicability decided by the separate independent-context gate in the new `l1-scoped-generalization` (SG-3), the two axes orthogonal (SG-11) so a rule may be ratified and **narrow** — normal and common, since the strictest conventions are usually the most local; no PC invariant relaxed, an unintended universality claim removed | Pattern codification — the disciplined memory-to-governance pathway by which a stable, dated behavioral pattern becomes an enforceable rule (distinct from memory consolidation, which abstracts within the corpus, and from EXT-7 skill distillation, patterns→capabilities; here patterns→rules): earned not automatic — recurrence is only a candidate signal, a binding rule additionally needs durability + explicit human ratification (PC-1); propose → ratify → write, the agent observes/proposes/writes and the human is the sole author of binding norms, never hand-editing the corpus and the agent never self-promoting (PC-2); tiered bindingness rising only with evidence — observation (non-binding) → advisory pattern → ratified rule (PC-3); recurrence detectable because observations are dated, the date the load-bearing unit of the promotion query (PC-4); rules carry ratification + last-review provenance, are periodically re-validated and reversibly demoted when contradicted, a rule is a standing hypothesis not a decree (PC-5); provisional/experimental patterns quarantined from direct promotion, graduated first (PC-6); auditable evidence-and-ratification pathway traceable back to the dated observations (PC-7); feeds governance without becoming it and never self-grants authority, composing SEC-10/LP-10 human-rooted authority (PC-8); generalizes SEC-9 permission-promotion to any behavioral norm; composes l1-memory-consolidation / l1-security / l2-agent-constitution / l1-practice-analytics / l1-operational-ledger; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-dialog DG-10 (promotable remembered decision); v1.0.2 links `l1-project-vocabulary` as a consumer of this pathway — a domain term earns canonical status through PC-1/PC-2 and retires through PC-5, and a generator never self-promotes a coinage (VOC-7) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |

| [l1-provenance-taint.md](specifications/l1-provenance-taint.md) | Provenance taint — the at-rest, persistent, propagating origin-trust taint on stored data, the missing "at-rest" complement to render-side trust-composition and the fifth provenance-family member ordered by data-life phase (origin-trust → derivation → authenticity → render-trust): taint captured at ingest by origin, internal-authored (first-party) vs external-synced (third-party) (PT-1); external/unknown untrusted by default, trust affirmative never assumed (PT-2); persistent + immutable-downward — the store never launders taint, downgrade free but upgrade untrusted→trusted human-rooted only, recorded (PT-3, composes SEC-10); propagates through every derived representation — a summary/entity/embedding/index inherits its source's taint, taint cannot be escaped by transformation (PT-4, rides data-lineage edges); most-untrusted-wins on combination, a single untrusted source taints the whole derivation (PT-5); sets runtime provenance on recall — a tainted memory recalled into a prompt is neutralized-as-data by default, feeding not duplicating the render-side contract (PT-6); inspectable + auditable enumeration of external-tainted content and its influence (PT-7); composes component-scanning + data-lineage, distinct from attestation-authenticity (PT-8); composes l1-context-provenance / l1-data-lineage / l1-component-scanning / l1-memory-intelligence / l1-security; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-language NL-17 (origin-taint provenance, never relaxing NL-11's safe default) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.1 |

| [l1-action-gating.md](specifications/l1-action-gating.md) ; v1.1.0 AG-9 — a gate is only a gate where the answer **can be given**: an authorization prompt raised on a channel the current caller cannot reach hangs the run instead of gating it, so the invocation declares its answering surface and the gate picks a mechanism that reaches it, refusing visibly and naming what could not be asked when none does; escalating to a prompt nobody sees and downgrading to skip the question are both closed; mechanism follows *who can answer*, tier follows consequence — the gate-side application of REA-5 ; v1.0.3 links `l1-consent-binding` - the question AG leaves unasked after assigning a tier: *what was the grant for, and when does it stop applying?* AG-7 records that an act was approved and by whom, not with enough precision to decide whether the next attempt is the same act; CB gives AG-6 de-escalation rules a bound identity (a resolved invocation, never a name or category) and a lapse rule - which matters most precisely where AG-5 correctly minimizes prompts | Action gating — authorization friction proportional to an action's consequence (the how-much-friction contract, distinct from the threat-guard that blocks malicious and from config-lockdown governance): friction proportional to consequence, uniform gating (everything/nothing) forbidden (AG-1); consequence classified by explicit legible axes — reversibility, blast radius, external visibility, value at stake (AG-2); closed ordered tier ladder auto/confirm/approval, tier = highest axis demand (AG-3); read-safe frictionless, irreversible/external/value never auto, unclassifiable fails to the higher tier (AG-4); friction-fatigue a first-class failure — over-gating is a defect not caution, blanket-approval trains rubber-stamping and nullifies the gate on what matters (AG-5); learned scoped revocable human-ratified de-escalation composing SEC-9, never self-escalation-bypass, top tier non-de-escalatable (AG-6); every gate decision auditable with tier + axes + approver (AG-7); governable stricter never silently laxer, top-tier floor un-removable composing policy-governance (AG-8); composes l2-tool-security (the guard that enforces the tier) / l1-interception-model / l1-policy-governance / l1-security (SEC-9 de-escalation) / l2-orchestration / l2-agent-autonomy; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-portability LP-16 (effect risk-class declaration) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |

| [l1-deep-research.md](specifications/l1-deep-research.md) | Deep research — autonomous investigation of an open-ended question over external, open, *discovered* sources ending in a claim-verified, source-attributed, coverage-honest report; the concept parent of the existing l2-deep-research engine: open discovered sources as an explored environment not an ingested payload (DR-1); bounded sub-question investigation tree reusing recursive decomposition (DR-2); gather→read→synthesize never gather-and-dump (DR-3); grounded claim-verified synthesis, coverage declared not faked (DR-4); untrusted-by-default fetched content neutralized at the boundary (DR-5); fixed budget with progress measured against the question not the fetch count (DR-6); monotonic finding accretion with an append-only keep/discard ledger (DR-7); frozen criteria + independently-gated faithfulness, no mid-run goalpost-moving and no self-grading (DR-8); autonomous never-stall operation within a hard ceiling (DR-9); observable cost-rolled-up resumable investigation (DR-10); attributed coverage-honest deliverable (DR-11); composes l1-recursive-decomposition / l1-claim-verification / l1-browser-control / l1-context-provenance / l1-loop-governance / l1-generation-budget / l1-data-lineage / l1-orchestration / l1-work-liveness; the disciplined-loop family (harness-engineering/optimization, loop-governance) owns the shared iterate-evaluate-keep/discard machinery — deep research composes it over a different subject (an external question, not the agent's own harness) rather than duplicating it | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-recursive-decomposition.md](specifications/l1-recursive-decomposition.md) | Recursive decomposition — the third stance on an over-window input beside compression (shrink) and progressive disclosure (defer), processing it in place by bounded recursive self/sub-calls over pieces so effective context exceeds the raw window: an over-window input is an external addressable environment the model navigates programmatically, not a payload swallowed whole (RD-1); recursive processing over bounded within-window pieces, a piece still too large decomposed again so every actual call sees a within-window slice (RD-2); combine map-then-reduce never re-concatenate, the reduce itself bounded (RD-3); bounded depth + breadth by construction, fail-fast on exceed, halt-and-surface an unboundable piece never force it through (RD-4); observable cost-rolled-up correlated tree — every recursive child a first-class parent/root-correlated run whose usage+cost roll up (RD-5); a third orthogonal stance chosen by input shape — compress redundant structure, disclose a catalog, decompose a heterogeneous whole (RD-6); deterministic replayable slicing attributable to pieces (RD-7); grounded reduction — coverage may be lost but never faked, unexamined regions declared not glossed (RD-8); composes l1-context-compression / l1-progressive-disclosure / l1-orchestration / l1-loop-governance / l1-generation-budget / l1-data-lineage / l1-claim-verification; the host-neutral nodus realization is l1-nodus-language NL-18 (bounded recursive `RUN` with mandatory MAX_DEPTH) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-tokenization-boundary.md](specifications/l1-tokenization-boundary.md) | Tokenization boundary — the frontier where the system's characters become the receiving model's symbol alphabet, and the contract every size/cut/cost/trust decision across that frontier must satisfy: the model's alphabet is the ground truth for size, cost, cut points and cache identity (TB-1); encoding is NOT composable — appending content retroactively re-encodes content before it, so a span's encoding is not a prefix of the whole's and any design that splits content, encodes the pieces independently and assumes they concatenate is wrong by construction (TB-2); cut only at seams the encoder declares stable, an unknown boundary being unstable, stability asserted never inferred (TB-3); control symbols (frame/turn/role/channel/stop markers) occupy a disjoint sub-alphabet structurally unreachable by encoding any content whatsoever — a property of the encoder not a sanitizer after it — which is also exactly why they are the only provably stable seams, the two properties being one fact seen from two sides (TB-4); minting a control symbol is a per-symbol authority held by trusted scaffolding and never by content, and the boundary's default for control-looking content is REFUSAL, with inert-encoding and minting each an explicit per-surface per-symbol disposition — neither silent inerting (hides an attack) nor silent promotion (is the attack) permitted (TB-5); the measure is the receiving model's own encoder and counts under different encoders are different quantities never substituted, compared, or aggregated (TB-6); an unidentifiable encoder fails loudly rather than defaulting, family rules are declared with stated reach, and a declared estimate never grounds an irreversible decision — no truncation, eviction, hard budget halt, or asserted cost figure rests on one (TB-7); an encoder is a versioned digest-verified self-validating artifact whose identity covers its control sub-alphabet, so two encoders sharing content symbols but differing in control symbols are different encoders and must not share a name, with structural invariants (bijective mapping, declared size, no duplicate ids) checked never assumed (TB-8); round-trip fidelity with declared — never silently default — lossy decode, and a truncated symbol sequence is not a text prefix so a streamed partial symbol must not be rendered as complete (TB-9); symbol↔source-span correspondence is reportable because symbol and character boundaries do not align (TB-10). Supplies the structural floor beneath l1-context-provenance CP-2/CP-6 (→ CP-9), the seam rule beneath l1-cache-stable-context CSC-2 (→ CSC-12) and l1-inference-cache IC-1/IC-9 (→ IC-10), and the measure beneath l1-generation-budget / l1-context-compression / trace-derived cost accounting; nodus realizations = l1-nodus-language NL-19 (unforgeable frame markers & seam-anchored segmentation) + l1-nodus-environment NE-14 (declared budget measure) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-session-reinforcement.md](specifications/l1-session-reinforcement.md) | Session reinforcement — host-side runtime control plane against in-session drift: typed advisory classes on declared classifier/threshold/state triggers (SR-1/SR-2); tighten-only direction monotonicity — an injection that would relax or contradict the standing baseline is invalid by construction, which makes a forged "system" advisory structurally detectable regardless of how authentic it looks (SR-3); host-only issuance — advisory-shaped content in any data channel keeps its provenance and is never honored (SR-4, composes context-provenance + SEC-10); long-horizon re-anchoring from a curated versioned baseline core, verbatim-or-curated never paraphrased (SR-5); supervisor probes — suspend-answer-resume, precedence over in-flight instructions within SR-3 bounds, no agent self-exit, answers recorded (SR-6); context-not-authority — no capability granted, no per-effect gate substituted (SR-7); budgeted, observable, non-nagging emission with declared re-issue policy (SR-8); delivered over the dynamic-harness environment-injection seam (DH-3/DH-5); nodus workflow side already covered by NL-11/NL-15/NL-19/LP-10 — no new language invariant needed | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-knowledge-horizon.md](specifications/l1-knowledge-horizon.md) | Knowledge horizon — generation-side anti-confabulation discipline (micro-spec): declared model knowledge horizon + host-injected current date as volatile context outside the stable prefix (KH-1); retrieval-before-assertion for unrecognized or post-horizon entities, honest unverified marking when retrieval is unavailable — recognizing a franchise is not knowing its latest member (KH-2); now-anchored query formulation + present-tense current-status routing (KH-3); post-retrieval trust calibration — retrieved evidence outranks parametric memory with declared skepticism classes requiring corroboration (KH-4); **KH-5 version-anchored dependency grounding** — parametric knowledge of a library is a version-blur skewed to the training-era-common release, so recognition ≠ knowing the pinned version's API surface; external-dependency usage is grounded against the pinned version's authoritative docs (resolved from the project's dependency/lock manifest via l1-code-intelligence), not parametric memory, and a recognized pre-horizon library still triggers grounding on version drift (different major/minor, renamed/deprecated API) else usage is marked unverified — extends KH-2 beyond the unrecognized/post-horizon trigger to the recognized-but-version-drifted case (the confident wrong-version API call); nodus realization = existing SchemaProvider schema-aware validation, no new NL invariant; the generation-side complement of l1-claim-verification (which audits claims already produced) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |
| [l1-generation-shaping.md](specifications/l1-generation-shaping.md) | Generation shaping — the third output-economy concern beside the length budget (l1-generation-budget) and the output contract, governing the *character & effort* of a turn's generation rather than its length: turn-classified modulation keyed on what the turn is — routine resume-after-tool vs fresh/error/ambiguous (GS-1); verbosity steering as a request-instruction that suppresses ceremony/preamble/restatement, never a post-hoc trim of produced output (GS-2); floored effort/reasoning-depth modulation — lowered toward a floor on demonstrably-routine turns, kept full on new/error/ambiguous/high-stakes (GS-3); correctness supremacy as the load-bearing floor — shaping never trades correctness/completeness/safety for brevity or speed, steps aside on any hard/failing/ambiguous signal (GS-4); cache-stable steering placement — the persistent directive sits at a stable seam so it never busts the KV cache it is trying to cheapen, composing CSC-2 (GS-5); counterfactual measurement honesty — output savings are unobservable so a report is an estimate+CI or a held-out control-group measured number, never a fabricated exact figure, applying solution-frugality FR-10 (GS-6); opt-in/off-by-default/reversible/live-reconfigurable with no cold restart (GS-7); learned-and-overridable level inferred from implicit behavioral signals (interrupts) via the user model UM-2/UM-4 (GS-8); accounted & observable as a distinct signal (GS-9); nodus mapping needs no new NL invariant — host-side over NL-15 volatile suffix + ModelProvider options + @err/!! + HO-8 cost classes | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-value-settlement.md](specifications/l1-value-settlement.md) | Outbound value settlement — paying an external party (API/service/charging peer) for value received, the third budget axis distinct from inbound usage-allowance and per-request generation-budget (VS-1); principal-set spending envelope (per-payment + cumulative ceiling, allowlisted recipients, rate limit) on the human-write-only authority plane, agent-read-only never agent-set (VS-2, SEC-10); bounded-authority custody — scoped spend the agent cannot exceed even if compromised, funding secret never in context (VS-3, SEC-1); every payment gated at the top of the action-gating ladder, allowlisted recipient a precondition not a waivable friction (VS-4, AG-1/AG-2/AG-4/AG-8); fail-closed — path down/envelope exhausted/denied blocks the paid action, never falls back to unmetered access or unbounded retry (VS-5, INT-3); bounded machine-negotiable payment-required handshake that settles once or aborts, never loops (VS-6); every payment provable via unforgeable receipt + operational ledger, unreceipted = unaccounted (VS-7, TR-4); opt-in/local-first/rail-neutral provider seam — core performs no payment and names no rail, peer payee = federation settlement case, enabling payment adds a capability not a server dependency (VS-8, deployment-neutrality/AF-5/AF-6); composes l1-security/l1-action-gating/l1-usage-allowance/l1-generation-budget/l1-tool-receipts/l1-operational-ledger/l1-interception-model/l1-agent-federation/l1-deployment-neutrality/l1-policy-governance; nodus realization = l1-nodus-portability LP-17; concept-only (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

| [l1-incremental-execution.md](specifications/l1-incremental-execution.md) | Incremental workflow re-execution — re-running a graph after a small change without redoing the work the change did not touch (the incremental-build discipline applied to a workflow graph): per-step output memoization under a content-addressed key over transitive input identity — own inputs + ancestry signature (IE-1); | [l1-observation-retention.md](specifications/l1-observation-retention.md) | Observation retention — how a continuously-growing operational record stays answerable at every horizon (last minute and last year) while consuming a bounded declared amount of local storage, without either keeping everything or deleting the long horizon exactly when a trend question needs it: one logical series retained simultaneously at a full-resolution recent tier and progressively coarser derived tiers with the layer selecting the tier and the consumer never naming one (OR-1); **aggregate sufficiency** — a coarse point carries sum, count, min, max, anomalous fraction, and covered interval, so average/min/max/anomaly-rate over any range are computed *exactly* from a coarse tier and only intra-interval shape is lost, a mean-only tier being forbidden because it makes extremes unrecoverable and systematically understates spikes (OR-2); **a gap is a value** — an interval where measurement was impossible records an explicit marker distinguishable from every real value including zero, with covered-vs-expected counts so quiet and not-observed are never conflated, zero-substitution / last-value-carry-forward / silent omission each forbidden as converting unavailability into a false statement (OR-3); budget-bounded not horizon-promised, with the actual retained horizon measured and reported and reclamation by whole-oldest-segment rotation (OR-4); append-only immutable history where a correction is a new observation never an edit (OR-5); coarse tiers derived incrementally and backfilled from the finest tier still holding the interval, two independent collections of one quantity forbidden since they can disagree (OR-6); every answer states the resolution actually used and carries an explicit truncation marker beyond the retained horizon, silent shortening and upsampling-to-look-fine-grained both forbidden (OR-7); working set bounded by active series and tier count, never by horizon or query range, long-range queries streaming (OR-8); retention local, inspectable, exportable, never silent (OR-9). | [l1-anomaly-consensus.md](specifications/l1-anomaly-consensus.md) | Anomaly consensus — always-on unsupervised per-series anomaly marking whose false-positive rate is controlled **structurally** rather than by per-series threshold tuning nobody has time to do: each series carries its own cheap models trained unlabelled on its own history with per-sample cost bounded independently of history length, many light models never one shared heavy one, no user content and no generative model as input (AC-1); **the unanimity gate** — several models per series trained on *different staggered* historical windows collectively spanning far more history than any single window all score every sample and a sample is marked anomalous only if **every** model agrees, a majority or weighted vote not being an anomaly, this being the layer's sole false-positive control and an explicit replacement for per-series tuning (AC-2); **the verdict is one flag carried inline with the observation itself** — not a parallel series, not a side table — so marking costs no additional storage and the anomaly *rate* over any range or grouping is the ordinary mean of that flag computed by the normal query path and preserved exactly through coarse retention tiers, value and verdict being one physical record that cannot drift apart (AC-3); anomaly rate first-class at two grains, per-series-over-time and per-unit-at-an-instant, both ordinary observable series in their own right (AC-4); **assistance not paging** — a mark never by itself raises a graded alert or notifies, escalation staying exclusively with the rule layer, only a sustained elevated *unit-level* rate surfacing as a low-urgency event, so notification volume is bounded by construction whatever the mark count (AC-5); unscored is never normal — an unscorable sample is marked unscored and excluded from rate denominators, so a cold or falling-behind system never appears healthy for lack of verdicts (AC-6); training and scoring out of the observation path, budgeted, degrading to unscored, and switchable off entirely (AC-7); explainable and reproducible verdicts — participating models, their training windows, their scores and thresholds, or the verdict is not shown (AC-8); concept drift handled by recorded periodic retraining so a durable regime change becomes the new normal rather than a permanent anomaly stream, the retraining boundary recorded so world-changed stays distinguishable from baseline-moved (AC-9); local-first — models, windows, verdicts never leave the device (AC-10). Nodus carrier = HO-16. Concept-only (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-change-attribution.md](specifications/l1-change-attribution.md) | Change attribution — the mechanism turning "something went wrong around 14:32" into "these twelve signals moved, ranked", the entry point of an investigation and never its conclusion: window-scoped ranked scoring over **all** eligible series in scope rather than a pre-chosen subset, output ordered with a score per series and never a yes/no, a design requiring the user to name candidates in advance defeating the purpose (CA-1); the comparison baseline **derived from the window itself** (a longer immediately-preceding interval) and always reported, so the operator selects one interval and never constructs a control, an explicitly supplied baseline permitted and then reported as supplied (CA-2); **two named scoring families** — distributional (distribution shift, sensitive to variance and shape changes at similar means) and magnitude (relative aggregate change, robust for sparse and on/off series) — both always available with the family and aggregation disclosed on every result, a single unnamed relevance number forbidden (CA-3); the same machinery ranks raw values **or** anomaly rates, which-signals-behaved-unusually being a distinct and often more discriminating question than which-signals-moved (CA-4); **shortlist never a cause** — the result is candidates for investigation, and presenting a rank as a cause, recording it as root cause, or feeding it to automated remediation are all forbidden since a wrong automatic cause stops the investigation (CA-5); bounded candidate set with an explicit truncation marker naming how many were dropped and by what rule, a silently truncated ranking being indistinguishable from a complete one (CA-6); read-only, non-perturbing, cancellable with partial progress, unable to starve the running system so the diagnosis never becomes the incident (CA-7); **refuse rather than answer weakly** — a too-short window or insufficient retained resolution is refused with the reason rather than answered with a low-confidence ranking that looks normal, with resolution and coverage stated on every result (CA-8); composable iterative narrowing by window, scope filter, quantity, and family with every run's parameters recorded so an investigation is reconstructable (CA-9); evaluated by the holder with the widest view, naming the evaluating holder and any series it could not see, partial coverage disclosed never silently narrowed (CA-10).
