| [l1-record-evolution.md](specifications/l1-record-evolution.md) | Record evolution — what a stored or transmitted record means to a reader that did not write it, and how its shape may change without destroying that meaning. **The reader population sets the regime, not the record's contents**: same-build, independently-shipped, or **re-publishing** — the strictest, where whatever a reader drops on the way through is *destroyed*, not ignored, and under content addressing a lossy pass mints a new identity for an unchanged cohort (REC-1/REC-2). An **interpreted core** versus an **opaque writer-private region** with strictly one-way promotion makes the free lane the default and keeps the committed surface small (REC-3); designated-slot **variant passthrough** retains the intact subtree and reclassifies variant *addition* to compatible, with tolerance explicitly not inherited by nested slots (REC-4); **residual capture** of unmodelled fields makes losslessness mechanical rather than a discipline future authors must remember, under an enumerated capture-level manifest whose identifiers are never reused (REC-5); tolerance is bounded to absent vocabulary and never to damage — a known-but-malformed variant still rejects (REC-6); **separate authoring and acceptance schemas**, because reading with the authoring schema makes the tolerant path unreachable in exactly the case it was written for, and unreachable in tests too (REC-7); refusal taken on the cheapest already-held signal before any fetch, making do-no-further-work structural instead of a remembered early return (REC-8); a minimum-reader declaration reserved for changes an older reader cannot safely *interpret*, never for preservation (REC-9); **openness decided by reader population**, with the observation that one strict leaf defeats a tolerant envelope and rejects the whole record (REC-10); a content address proves the bytes while a separate cross-check proves they mean what the referrer assumed (REC-11); a **frozen wire-form guard that fails on all drift including compatible additions**, generated never hand-edited, with the wire form projected explicitly and the projection itself asserted since a frozen surface can be frozen and wrong (REC-12); record/contract/distribution versions independent (REC-13). The persistence twin of l1-peer-compatibility (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-resumable-subscription.md](specifications/l1-resumable-subscription.md) | Resumable subscription — the contract between a durable **ordered log** and a subscriber that will disconnect and come back (session transcripts, agent inboxes, activity feeds, run traces, communication views). Append-only immutable records give a durable position identity (RSB-1); the **durable write is the source of truth and live notification is only a wake-up**, so a dropped notification costs a reconnect and never an event (RSB-2); the record's own position **is** the resume cursor — no side-channel sequence, no window re-request, no de-duplication — and the cursor tracks what was *applied*, not received (RSB-3); the guarantee is precisely bounded to exactly-once, ascending, gap-free above the cursor **for representable records**, and nothing else (RSB-4); an unrepresentable record is **skipped and the cursor advances**, because holding it stalls the subscription permanently behind one row and retrying loops — a negotiated version is the real remedy, the skip the interim (RSB-5); therefore **a position gap is not evidence of loss** and must never be retried, since the skip is deterministic and recurs (RSB-6); **framing carries no world semantics** — a bounded initial batch is a transport decision, batch-ended is not caught-up, and novelty derives from record content and subscriber state, never from which frame carried a row (RSB-7); closed vocabulary only where an unknown value makes the record unreadable, open where it merely annotates, which decides whether an addition costs a version (RSB-8); a cursor predating retention is reported explicitly, distinctly from nothing-new (RSB-9); independent logs merged consumer-side with no invented global order, safe exactly because causally related records share one log (RSB-10); per-source degradation so one failing source never darkens a multi-source view (RSB-11). Demarcated from the in-process event mesh (EM-9), retention, work liveness and device sync (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-inverse-derivation.md](specifications/l1-inverse-derivation.md) | Inverse derivation — running the production arrow **backwards** over work the office did not produce: from delivered artifacts to the units of work, the plan, the framing, and the originating intent, using the work's own record as evidence. The **episode** as the unit of reconstruction — a completed arc with a recoverable before-state, an after-state, executable checks, and a trajectory — never a whole product, which has no checkable starting point, no bounded cost, and no possibility of replay (IVD-1); every recovered link graded **attested / inferred / assumed**, with grades non-inheriting along the chain, because the most confident-sounding parts of a reconstruction are typically its least supported — nobody wrote down what nobody thought (IVD-2); the **trajectory as first-class output** — attempts, reversals, abandonments, ordering — on the grounds that the finished artifact already shows the answer and only the record shows the search (IVD-3); reconstruction derived, re-derivable, never authoritative over or mutating its source (IVD-4); an explicit human-edited registry of **pinned, fingerprinted** entries carrying its own version, closing the silent-corpus-drift hole where a maintainer rewrites the history a revision points at and every measurement taken against it is invalidated with no error reported — on mismatch the entry is **suspended** and dependent measurements marked unsound, never silently re-pinned to whatever the source now holds (IVD-5); automated discovery permitted to **propose** candidates and forbidden to admit them (IVD-6); selection on **record quality, not product popularity** — proxy metrics rank product success, which is near-uncorrelated with reconstructability, so a proxy-ranked corpus fills with exactly the sources that cannot be reconstructed (IVD-7); mechanical prefilter plus an irreducible human judgment on sampled episodes (IVD-8); foreign record content treated as untrusted data under CP-1/CP-2, since reconstruction reads adversarial material by design (IVD-9); thin records degrading honestly with reported coverage rather than narrative gap-filling (IVD-10); output abstracted to **method**, verbatim carry-over refused through the ORI-1/ORI-3 gate (IVD-11); materialized corpora offline-complete (IVD-12); bounded runs naming the links they did not reach (IVD-13). Demarcated from code comprehension (artifact vs process), `l1-reproduction-recipe` (recorded by the producer vs asserted by a reader), and `l1-specialty-exemplars` (authored vs recovered) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-capability-leveling.md](specifications/l1-capability-leveling.md) | Capability leveling — the closed loop that **narrows the spread between models on long-horizon construction work** by replaying reconstructed episodes and converting each divergence into a durable, transferable correction. Objective stated as **spread collapse at a bar, never reproduction**, with similarity-to-exemplar explicitly rejected as an optimization target since it selects for imitating one body of work (LVL-1); a measured **two-part marginal stopping rule** — the weakest performer stopped rising and the spread stopped narrowing — replacing the non-terminating "iterate until identical", which is unreachable because two competent engineers never produce identical work — and reporting **which of two endings** it reached, converged at the bar or **plateaued below it**, the latter being the loop's most misleading state since a collapsed spread among models that all fail puts the headline metric at its best exactly where nothing was achieved (LVL-2); comparison over outcome **and recorded trajectory**, with an implausibly straight path recorded as a **contamination signal rather than an outstanding result** — the structural answer to a framing reconstructed from finished work already knowing the answer (LVL-3); mandatory four-way cause classification of every divergence — knowledge / capability / framing / **arbitrary** (LVL-4); arbitrary divergences discarded with an explicit **retention bias** on uncertain calls, justified by asymmetry: a wrongly-kept lesson is visible and falsifiable, a wrongly-discarded one is silently gone (LVL-5); **carrier chosen by who executes the lesson** — the five-rung ladder from enforced check through enforced procedure, triggered instruction, and standing rule to precedent — because filing every lesson as instruction puts it in the highest-variance carrier for the problem *defined* by execution variance (LVL-6); executable carriers gated on an **objective oracle**, judgment lessons kept instructional rather than converted into mechanical proxies that get optimized against (LVL-7); hardening refused where it degrades strong performers, forbidding leveling by ceiling loss (LVL-8); laboratory isolation defined by **where carriers are materialized**, since retrieval does not read draft markers (LVL-9); a **single human ratification gate on the assembled body of lessons** at the boundary all lessons cross, per-lesson ratification rejected as rubber-stamping that reads as authority while providing none (LVL-10); scope earned across **independent** contexts, which is what gives varied technologies and domains their instrumental role and what stops one exemplar's house style being promoted to universal practice (LVL-11); evidentiary grades inherited from the reconstruction and surviving aggregation (LVL-12); **one learning machinery** — a new source of experience for the existing distillation surfaces, never a second store whose winner is decided by retrieval accident (LVL-13); **context plane only**, no weight adaptation, canonical lessons rendered per host with honest degradation (LVL-14); budgeted runs reporting unreached episodes and models rather than averaging partial rounds as complete (LVL-15); episode de-duplication before measurement, so a corpus does not measure one situation repeatedly and report it as breadth (LVL-16); measurements comparable only within a named corpus version (LVL-17). Demarcated from `l1-simulation`, `l1-evaluation-suites`, `l1-model-benchmarking`, `l1-specialty-exemplars`, and `l1-competitive-execution` | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-evidence-archive.md](specifications/l1-evidence-archive.md) | Evidence archive — the durable, immutable, device-local record of **what was said and done**, kept beneath every reduction that acts on it. Resolves the asymmetry in which a user's deliberate rewind is non-destructive (RW-3) while the system's automatic compaction is not: archive-before-reduce as a precondition, not a side effect (EA-1); reduction artifacts become **addresses** into the archive rather than replacements for it, turning compaction into an indexing operation (EA-2); expansion on demand as an ordinary capability, not a recovery procedure (EA-3); append-only immutability with supersession instead of edits (EA-4); removal only by explicit user act or declared budget, always reported, never a silent thinning or a plausible partial (EA-5); device-local and never a source for any outbound path (EA-6); secret-safe on the way **out**, not on the way in — a redacting write destroys the fidelity the record exists for without un-happening the secret (EA-7); queryable with an honest horizon, OR-7's discipline on the non-numeric plane (EA-8); four-plane separation — evidence (said/done) vs memory (learned) vs ledger (decided) vs diagnostics (machinery) (EA-9); evidence never ambient, the archive is what makes keeping *less* in context safe (EA-10); provenance survives the round trip so storage is not laundering (EA-11); cheap, bounded, non-fatal — an archive failure fails the reduction, never the turn (EA-12). Claims the non-numeric retention plane `l1-observation-retention` scopes out and no other plane owned (diagnostics ephemeral DL-5, memory pruned MEM-5, receipts session-bound TR-5); main-only host concept (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-fanout-attestation.md](specifications/l1-fanout-attestation.md) | Fan-out attestation - **concurrency is a claim, and a claim needs evidence**: a coordinator that launches one worker, waits, then launches the next produces the same artifacts, logs and completion report as one that genuinely fanned out, so the failure is invisible until a deadline is missed. The **launch barrier**: an episode declares its exact member set, records a **host-issued** launch handle per member, and **seals before the first wait** (FAN-2) - an ordering the serial launch-wait-launch pattern structurally cannot satisfy; handles are host-issued, never coordinator-authored, and a foreground result recorded as a start is a forged attestation (FAN-3); a partial launch keeps the episode **open** and ends in retry or a reasoned **terminal, non-successful abandonment**, never a seal around the gap (FAN-4); the attestation **bounds its own claim** to acceptance ordering and proves nothing about wall-clock overlap, write isolation, worker honesty, or integration (FAN-5); a return is scheduler completion, not verification (FAN-6); rolling episodes bounded by host concurrency, results reported in **declared** order not completion order (FAN-7); a **declared sequential fallback** where the host cannot fan out, never a parallel description of a serial run (FAN-8); native scheduling over detached process farms that surrender cancellation/observability/cost attribution, with no episode opened where no per-member identity exists (FAN-9); append-only episode state refusing impossible transition sequences (FAN-10); check-level parallelism excluded from attestation (FAN-11). **Orthogonal** to the coordination family - parallel-staffing, competitive-execution and deliberation each decide *what* is fanned out and *how fan-in resolves*, none governs the launch act, and all three can claim a concurrency they did not perform. Nodus realization: NL-24 (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-acceptance-oracle.md](specifications/l1-acceptance-oracle.md) ; v1.1.0 AO-12/13 — a measurable criterion **names its counterfeits** (clip, hide, move the excess outside the measured region, shrink the thing being measured) and its **repair precedence**, since the cheapest repair is nearly always the one that damages the property the measurement stands for; and a threshold **belongs to a class** — one bound over a heterogeneous population fails loudly on the legitimate outliers while the real defect sits inside the tolerance they forced, an item whose measure is undefined for its class is **excluded and recorded as excluded** rather than scored by a formula that does not apply, and an authored override is a repair control that explains provenance, never an exemption that waives the bound | Acceptance oracle integrity - the criterion that decides *done* is **the one artifact in the verification stack that nothing checks**, and every layer beneath it faithfully certifies whatever it says. Distinguished sharply from CMP-4 (which forbids a *weaker but real* proxy): this forbids an instrument **structurally incapable of failing**, and moves enforcement to **authoring time**. Criteria precede the work and are what completion is judged against (AO-1); one **observable outcome** per criterion, never an activity that cannot be failed (AO-2); **runnable or judged, declared** - a half-bound criterion is refused, never silently downgraded (AO-3); success signalled only by what success can produce, requiring **both** successful termination and a distinctive signal, since failure output uses ok/done/passed/0 as readily (AO-4); an **absence claim needs a positive control** - untested, it is indistinguishable from a check looking in the wrong place, the most common way a green result carries no information (AO-5); a supplied figure is derived from the source of truth, never restated as its own proof (AO-6); a **mandatory-as-a-step, advisory-per-finding, non-executing falsifiability review** catching the mechanical classes before work starts, usable as a criterion of its own (AO-7); a mostly-judged set is a **disclosed condition**, not mechanical verification wearing a checklist's clothes (AO-8); judged criteria carry consequence-proportional evidence and are **never assumed low-risk** - checkability and consequence are independent axes (AO-9); an empty or malformed set is **refused, never satisfied** - *nothing to check* must never reduce to *everything checks out* (AO-10); stable explicit criterion identities, never position-derived (AO-11) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |
| [l1-consent-binding.md](specifications/l1-consent-binding.md) | Consent binding - the identity and lifetime of a grant, the question action-gating leaves unasked after deciding *how much* friction an act deserves. Consent binds the **fully resolved invocation** (operation, expanded arguments, location, interpreter, ambient name-resolution environment, limits, platform), never a name, category, tool, or intention (CB-1); the approver is shown the **resolved** form, since consent for the authored text is consent for an act the system will not perform, and the difference is exactly where the dangerous cases live (CB-2); **any change to a bound element lapses the grant** - no partial reuse, no close-enough match (CB-3); an unmatched act is **disclosed and stopped**, a property of the unmatched state and never a permanent dry-run mode (CB-4); consent is **authorization, never containment** - the permitted act keeps its full ambient reach, and presenting a grant as isolation misleads exactly the person being asked to decide (CB-5); the **coverage boundary is disclosed, not implied** - a grant does not reach called scripts, fixtures, or dependencies, so a byte-identical act runs under an old grant when its inputs change, and a workflow needing that enforced puts the inputs' identity *inside* the bound invocation (CB-6); grant records are principal-owned, stored outside the material they authorize, integrity-checked, and **fail closed** (CB-7); reviewed material is **data**, never instruction, and can never cause its own approval or install its own approver (CB-8); consent is **never evidence of correctness** (CB-9); grants and lapses are both auditable, the lapse naming the changed element (CB-10); grants are narrowable by the principal and **never self-widening** by the holder or the act (CB-11). Scopes AG-6 learned de-escalation, so the mechanism spending least approver attention is the most exact about what it covers (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-context-transition.md](specifications/l1-context-transition.md) | Context transition - **which disposition of a working context, and when taking one is legitimate at all**: the decision the token-economy family (compression, summarization, handoff, delegation) specifies transforms for and never makes. Every transition converts a **primary source** into a secondary one and what it loses is the *why* - an artifact records what was decided, never the alternatives rejected on the way (CXT-1); the decision belongs **at a work boundary and nowhere else**, so a budget threshold *signals* and never *fires*, because a mid-chunk reduction removes exactly what the work is still using (CXT-2); a closed, **cost-ordered** disposition set - continue / discard / externalize / delegate / compress - first admissible wins, no reordering and no sixth rung (CXT-3); continue is examined first as the only free move (CXT-4); discard requires **positive evidence of irrelevance**, absence of a reason to keep is not evidence (CXT-5); externalize only for a real crossing - host, location, principal, forked side thread (CXT-6); delegate only what runs unattended and returns a report, the one rung leaving the caller's primary source intact (CXT-7); compression is the default and **never the first reach**, bound by being directed at what the next chunk needs (CXT-8); hiding later work needs a real context boundary - an inline call leaves them resident and clears nothing (CXT-9); a declared **sharp-reasoning band**, transition taken at the nearest boundary before it is exhausted (CXT-10); every transition recorded with what it converted (CXT-11); the order is the contract, the verdict is not claimed mechanical (CXT-12) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-capability-reachability.md](specifications/l1-capability-reachability.md) ; v1.2.0 REA-12 — **absent / present-but-unconfigured / present-and-reachable** are three verdicts and a declaration marker is none of them: absence is a choice skipped quietly while installed-but-unconfigured is a defect reported loudly with the missing step named; an inherited environment marker proves only that a declaration propagated, and an artifact outliving its owner (a stale socket, lock, or registration) is *absent owner*, never presence ; v1.1.0 REA-11 added - reachability is a property of the **(capability, executor) pair** wherever more than one executor may do the work: REA-1 answers whether an actor may start a capability, not **which** actor holds it, so a plan naming a capability names the executor it was **established on** (establishing it on the commissioning side proves nothing about the side that will run it), **asymmetry is recorded rather than discovered at handover** and is a legitimate routing reason, and a capability never exercised in the **invocation mode it will actually run in** - typically a non-interactive/headless mode that loads and resolves differently from the interactive one - is an assumption, not an establishment; discovery of what an executor holds is **input to a plan, never an activation** | Capability reachability - **who may invoke a capability**, the property progressive disclosure and the extension-point model both presume and neither declares. A closed two-class partition: **agent-reachable** (resident descriptor, actor may initiate, composable by other capabilities, standing context cost) versus **human-only** (no descriptor, only a person, reachable by nothing else including by naming it, zero standing cost); undeclared defaults to agent-reachable (REA-1); two budgets of which only one is minimized - standing context is minimized, a person's **recall is spent deliberately** as the price of human agency, and treating it as waste converts every human-only capability and hands away the judgement it kept (REA-2); the class test is **autonomy, not reuse** - reuse justifies extraction, never reach (REA-3); human-only capabilities are structurally uncomposable, so material two of them share lives in **neither** and is externalized (REA-4); a human-only precondition is phrased as an action for the person, never attempted, since an attempt diagnoses a broken surface where a person's action is missing (REA-5); composition is an explicit invocation act naming one target, never a bare mention nor a path into internals (REA-6); a router hints and cannot fire, is updated in the same change as any reachable-capability change, and a stale one is a **correctness defect** read by the person least able to catch it (REA-7); precondition classes - **hard** (output wrong) carries a remediation pointer, **soft** (output blunt) carries none (REA-8); the discoverable set is declared once and rendered, never restated per surface (REA-9); a class change is an announced surface change (REA-10) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.2.0 |
| [l1-rejection-memory.md](specifications/l1-rejection-memory.md) | Rejection memory - the durable record of **declined proposals**, closing the re-proposal loop every recurring survey and every fresh session reopens. Keyed by **concept, never by request**, so the store scales with decisions rather than traffic (REJ-1); a **deferral is not a rejection** - circumstance expires, an entry does not, and recording one permanently closes what nobody decided against (REJ-2); **already-satisfied is not rejected** - closed by pointing at where the capability lives, since an entry there contaminates matching with a false rejection about the project's own contents (REJ-3); matched by concept, not wording, the false-negative direction being the expensive one (REJ-4); a match is **surfaced and re-asked, never auto-applied**, resolving to affirm / reconsider / distinguish (REJ-5); accumulated **independent demand** rides on the entry and is the strongest reconsideration signal there is (REJ-6); the store is read at **generation** time, not only at decision time (REJ-7); reconsideration retires the entry while previously closed requests stay closed (REJ-8); proposals only - a declined defect is never an entry, or the store becomes where broken things are argued away (REJ-9); entries are authored to be **applied cold** (REJ-10); authored on the decider's authority, never by the party that would otherwise do the work (REJ-11); an entry is **not** a generator constraint until deliberately promoted into one under the exclusion contract (REJ-12) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-instruction-economy.md](specifications/l1-instruction-economy.md) ; v1.1.0 IEC-12 — a tiered instruction set **names the condition that opens each deeper tier, and the condition is a trigger, not an appetite**: "read this when you need more detail" is unfalsifiable, so a cautious actor reads everything and the tiering restores the full standing cost while adding a second home for the same rule to drift; the primary path enumerates positively which files are read for an ordinary task, and a failure-count trigger states the count so escalation is a fact about the work rather than a judgement about how stuck the actor feels | Instruction economy - the **sentence-grain** member of the anti-bloat family, below harness composition (components) and capability reachability (descriptors). A line earns its place by **changing behaviour against the actor's default**, and plausibility is not the test (IEC-1); the no-op verdict is **actor-relative, settled by running rather than arguing, and expires when the actor changes** - a swapped model has a different default, so the set is re-measured, never inherited (IEC-2); a failing line is deleted whole, since trimming a no-op yields a shorter no-op (IEC-3); the environment is a source of truth and an instruction restating it is a **cache of a lookup** - cache what cannot be looked up: the unwritten convention, the reason, the trap no declaration confesses (IEC-4); duplication and **scattering** are opposite failures with opposite fixes, removal versus co-location (IEC-5); standing rules are placed with the role that has **attention to spare**, since a body of standards loaded onto the actor exploring, writing and debugging is dropped exactly when the work is hardest (IEC-6); steer by the target behaviour, since a bare prohibition makes the forbidden thing *more* available (IEC-7); density is bought with terms the actor already holds, a coinage is paid for in definition at every use (IEC-8); a completion criterion is both **checkable** and **demanding**, and its demand is the legwork it produces (IEC-9); accumulation is the default fate, so pruning is **scheduled** and a review producing only additions has not run (IEC-10); length is a failure mode independent of per-line quality, and its remedy is structural (IEC-11) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.1.0 |
| [l1-concept-grounding.md](specifications/l1-concept-grounding.md) | Concept grounding - the undeclared dependency structure inside every artifact a reader traverses **in order** (a report, a walkthrough, an onboarding path, a generated explanation). Each unit declares what it **requires** and what it **establishes** (GRD-1); the dependency is the **concept, never its name**, so a passage with no unfamiliar vocabulary can still lose the reader and a term scan explicitly does not satisfy the check (GRD-2); two grounding origins only - **assumed** (brought by the audience, fixed and declared) and **established** (landed by an earlier unit); *mentioned* is not grounded, and the set is monotone, so a second establishment is a bug report on the first (GRD-3); a unit is admissible only when all its requirements are grounded, and proceeding anyway is not an option because the cost compounds downstream (GRD-4); the grounded set **travels with the position and is never inferred by reading back**, which is what makes an ordered artifact compatible with concurrent, bounded-context production (GRD-5); a term is landed with its idea in one unit, since a name alone grounds nothing while appearing to (GRD-6); exactly two remedies for an ungrounded requirement - establish before, or promote to an assumption, the second being a **change of audience** (GRD-7); the assumption set is published in the artifact (GRD-8); termination is by **destination, not by exhaustion of source material**, and leftovers are expected (GRD-9); a gap in the source is named and resolved by supplying or cutting, never by plausible invention every downstream unit inherits (GRD-10) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-exploratory-planning.md](specifications/l1-exploratory-planning.md) | Exploratory planning - the phase **before** TG-1's precondition holds, where no requirements artifact exists and none can yet be written (a greenfield effort, a large migration, a subsystem whose shape is undecided). Output is **decisions, not deliverables**, and the phase's completion tell is the **urge to build**, which signals the route is clear rather than a temptation to resist (XPL-1); the **destination is named first and fixes the scope**, supplying the termination condition and the basis for every boundary (XPL-2); the decision unit completes on a **recorded answer**, sized to one session, typed by how it resolves - investigate / make-something-to-react-to / converse / unblock-by-doing - with the acting type earning its place only by unblocking a decision (XPL-3); **a unit needing a human is never completed by the actor supplying the human's side** - an actor that asks its own questions and answers them has fabricated the counterparty, detected by a record containing only the actor's contributions or an answer attributed to nobody, and where no person is available the honest outcome is an unresolved unit (XPL-4); deliberate incompleteness whose admission test is **whether the question can be stated sharply now, not whether it can be answered** - sharp-but-blocked is a unit, not-yet-sharp is fog, and pre-slicing fog manufactures precision that does not exist (XPL-5); fog graduates one patch at a time and leaves the fog region as it does (XPL-6); **out of scope is terminal, is not fog, and is not part of the route** - scope not sharpness lands it there, a mis-scoped unit is closed to leave the frontier unambiguously, and it stays out of the decisions record because a scope boundary is not a step on the walked route (XPL-7); the map is an **index, not a store** (XPL-8); claim before work, frontier = open + unblocked + **unclaimed** (XPL-9); structure in the host's **native relations** so the frontier renders where people already look (XPL-10); human-facing renderings **name** units, identifiers riding inside names (XPL-11); completion **hands off** by collapsing into the artifact TG-1 names, since feeding units straight to an executor discards the linked reasoning and leaves execution working from titles (XPL-12) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-foreign-agent-invocation.md](specifications/l1-foreign-agent-invocation.md) | Foreign agent invocation - the **commissioned** direction of the foreign-tool relationship, the one nothing owned: the marketplace governs what comes **in**, host-native rendering governs what goes **out**, artifact-derived observation governs what is **read afterwards** out-of-band having asked for nothing - none covers *this system authoring the order, starting a foreign agent runtime, and owning what it produced*. Centre of gravity is one property: **a foreign runtime's failures are predominantly silent, and its deviant paths do not default the way its primary path advertises**, so every failure here is shaped like success. The work order is the entire brief since the delegate holds zero shared state, and an order that cannot be written without making the decisions is design, which does not delegate (FAI-1); **containment posture asserted per call, never inherited** - a launch and a continuation are independent surfaces, a flag one accepts the other rejects, and a rejected flag falls back to an ambient configuration this system does not own, so an unassertable posture refuses the call (FAI-2); every idle input channel closed at launch, the failure being not an error but a process waiting forever on an unterminated input at zero load, indistinguishable from long work (FAI-3); **the deadline comes from the operation, not the caller's default** - a short ceiling kills real work and misreports it as the work's failure, no ceiling is an unbounded silent wait, and a trip is a failed run never a blind re-invocation of possibly-partial effects (FAI-4); a continuation names its **captured** session identity, never an implicit most-recent selector, because a wrong-target continuation returns a coherent well-formed result about different work (FAI-5); resolved configuration disclosed before the first billable call, since it is assembled from ambient files the principal may not know they wrote (FAI-6); **success decided by the produced artifact**, never a quiet diagnostic channel or a bare exit - healthy runs emit benign noise and every failure this contract exists for can exit cleanly (FAI-7); write authority requires a **recorded pre-state**, the diff against it being the only instrument of attribution and the only route to reversal for a black-box actor, so an unrecordable location is a stop not a warning (FAI-8); the delegate's report is a claim - the delegator reads the whole difference and **re-executes the checks itself** (FAI-9, CMP-3/CMP-8/AO-6); delegation bounded by a **floor** below which the delegator acts directly and a **ceiling** above which it takes the work over rather than escalating a cost it was engaged to absorb (FAI-10); capability established per **(capability, executor)** pair and in the invocation mode it will actually run in (FAI-11, REA-11); out-of-band completion announced before it is acted on, being the visible edge of a state transition that otherwise has none (FAI-12); everything the delegate emits is untrusted data (FAI-13, ADO-9/CB-8); demarcated against l1-host-native-rendering (authority-neutral outbound write) and l1-artifact-derived-observation (asked for nothing, derived never authoritative); nodus mapping - the deferred external step is the language-grain launch/return shape, and its missing piece is a declared deadline (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.1 |
| [l1-remote-liveness-verdict.md](specifications/l1-remote-liveness-verdict.md) | Remote liveness verdict - answering **is the remote entity still alive, or did we just lose the wire** honestly, closed to a three-state verdict never a boolean: **alive / unverifiable / confirmed-gone**, with *unverifiable* structurally distinct and never collapsible into either neighbour (RLV-1); confirmed-gone requires **positive evidence sourced from the party that actually holds the resource** - a timeout, closed socket, or local lookup miss is unverifiable by construction regardless of what field it is written into (RLV-2); **correlated silence across every entity behind one channel is diagnosed once, at the channel**, never multiplied into independent per-entity deaths (RLV-3); a termination signal is trusted only when it names the entity's **current incarnation**, generalizing WL-2's claim-release compare-and-clear from work ownership to liveness verdicts (RLV-4); a returned status is checked against **durable state**, never trusted on its own report (RLV-5); artifact evidence is stronger than a liveness signal but proves only the **narrower claim it actually names** - a matched commit proves the commit arrived, not that this run produced it or nothing later is missing (RLV-6); disconnection is **not by default termination**, the applicable policy and its schedule are disclosed rather than assumed, and losing the control plane is distinguished from losing the underlying execution (RLV-7); and **one physical resource carries exactly one liveness identity at a time** - two concurrent locus registrations of the same machine split its inventory and answer differently depending on which one is asked (RLV-8, extends LOC's substitution model); demarcated from l1-work-liveness (progress staleness assuming an intact channel, vs. existence classification when the channel itself is in question) and from l1-foreign-agent-invocation (one-shot commissioned execution vs. a continuously-reachable remote host classified repeatedly over its life) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.1 |
| [l1-cooperative-status-projection.md](specifications/l1-cooperative-status-projection.md) | Cooperative status projection - normalizing **many concurrently-running, vendor-foreign coding-agent CLIs**, each with its own incomplete hook contract, into one closed-vocabulary status feed: status is read from the vendor's cooperative hook channel, **never a terminal title or output heuristic**, and always resolves to a small closed vendor-neutral set (ASP-1); | [l1-signal-silencing.md](specifications/l1-signal-silencing.md) | Signal silencing — the alerting/suppression layer FL-9 names and hands off, specified for the first time: a silence withholds **delivery**, never observation, recording, counting, or rate-tracking, the sole exception being a class the **emitter declared ephemeral at emission** (SIL-1); the key is **resolved against real occurrences before the silence is accepted**, since a silence that matches nothing is listed, reports success, and delivers every future occurrence — a phantom indistinguishable from a working one, and the derivation runs from the matcher's identity, never the display form (SIL-2); breadth **disclosed at set time in the person's own terms**, never silently widened from one name to a class whose next genuine failure is the one undelivered (SIL-3); every silence offered with its reversal and the active set listable, so it is never a one-way door (SIL-4); bounded by time or condition, unbounded explicit and attributed (SIL-5); **never offered in place of a fix within reach**, and never before the diagnosis it would suppress (SIL-6, composing OWN-1); punch-through decided on attributes the sender **cannot profitably forge** — a self-declared urgency is a claim by the interested party and a shared channel converges on maximum severity (SIL-7); what was withheld is reviewable, or a delivery decision has become a data-loss decision (SIL-8); escalation survives silence unless the silence explicitly says otherwise, so being known is not a hiding place (SIL-9); instance / rule / global are three objects with three blast radii and never one control (SIL-10) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

observed output as the only evidence, with the specification explicitly **not** evidence because the gap between it and behaviour is the measurement (USM-5); **perturbation as scenario content**, with an eleven-class catalog of situation developments — reversal, abandonment, return, repetition, malformed and hostile input, interruption, concurrency, scale, legacy world, surface crossing — and a scenario declaring none labelled a smoke test (USM-6); tier and bound assigned at derivation so **the plan carries the cost of verifying itself**, an unaffordable set being a planning finding resolved by re-tiering or narrowing scope (SD-7); derivation **proposes** and the persona/goal/vantage are confirmed at the design gate, a disowned persona being the cheapest correction in the lifecycle and available exactly once (SD-8); the corpus belongs to the **delivered project** and runs with the office and its planning artifacts entirely absent (SD-9) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-remedy-authority.md](specifications/l1-remedy-authority.md) | Remedy authority — the unclaimed passage from a **finding** to a **change**, and the self-application contract `l1-improvement-loop` §5 deferred as "a separate, future contract with its own authority model": authority recorded per IMP-1 class and never globally (RA-1); a remedy applied only once the pin exists **and fails**, converting USM-12's blanket self-repair prohibition into a precondition because a pin is evidence that does not perish — and unmeetable by construction for a finding whose subject is not built, which caps plan-grain findings at *propose* (RA-2); whatever judged the finding immutable to the act remedying it, LG-3's spine at the finding grain (RA-3); a class that can pin nothing never reaching automatic, since the four non-defect classes violated no contract so no check can fail for them (RA-4); the closed rung ladder report/propose/confirm/automatic/chain (RA-5); the chain grant as an **enumerated**, independently-ceilinged pass stopping before anything irreversible (RA-6); every remedy reversible and ledgered append-only (RA-7); never verified by a check it authored (RA-8); authority never self-widening, recurrence being evidence for a human rather than an escalation trigger (RA-9); report the floor and silence never consent (RA-10) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-plan-rehearsal.md](specifications/l1-plan-rehearsal.md) | Plan rehearsal — playing the plan out **before the code exists**, the seam-side companion to scenario derivation's journey side: the subject is the relation between planned units — hand-off, shared state, ordering, concurrency, failure propagation — and nothing executes, so the play-out is reasoned from the plan's declarations (PR-1); findings filed against the **decomposition** while requirement defects keep SD-3's separate upward path (PR-2); every seam played with the upstream unit failing, partial, slow or repeated, the finding naming **who owns the recovery** (PR-3); findings falsifiable against what the plan does and does not say, so an invented seam is visible as one (PR-4); bound declared and unreached seams named, since "examined and sound" must not render like "never looked at" (PR-5); reports but never edits the plan (PR-6); an unrehearsable seam is itself the finding, never a skipped seam or an invented declaration (PR-7); closes the Plan stage before the design gate, because afterwards only the seams that were built can be examined (PR-8); versioned with the plan, re-rehearsed on drift with silent survivors flagged (PR-9) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-whole-system-rehearsal.md](specifications/l1-whole-system-rehearsal.md) | Whole-system rehearsal — the **assembled system** as a subject nothing previously owned, swept on a declared cadence and on demand: two modes each keeping its own effects discipline intact — **usage** with real effects in a disposable world, **mechanism** with effects suppressed — because suppression preserves the subject in one and destroys it in the other (WSR-1); the subject being behaviour present only when everything runs at once, not component checks re-run together (WSR-2); declared cadence and bound, conditional and never a per-change gate (WSR-3); an unattended sweep never blocking, delaying or competing with the user's real work (WSR-4); neither mode's outcome inferred from the other's (WSR-5); reports but never repairs, since repair destroys the baseline the next sweep compares against (WSR-6); comparability declared via an explicit baseline — corpus, catalog, composition, bounds (WSR-7); requested and fired sweeps the same contract (WSR-8); an incomplete sweep reporting partially and naming what went undecided (WSR-9) (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |
| [l1-uninformed-actor.md](specifications/l1-uninformed-actor.md) | Uninformed actor — the office role (`user-actor`, called *the user*) whose qualification is **not knowing**, closing the what/when/who triad with the usage-simulation instrument and planning-time scenario derivation. Defined by its **exclusions** — specification, source, plan, rationale, prior findings, the office's own user model — never by a capability list, since a definition written as capabilities has described a tester under another name (UIA-1); denial enforced by **staffing and reachability, never by instruction**, which is the reason the role exists at all: an agent cannot comply with an order to forget and its non-compliance is silent, confident, and indistinguishable from success — with a mandatory declaration wherever a host cannot restrict reachability, because an absolute with no disclosed fallback is one that gets claimed rather than met (UIA-2); **failure is the deliverable**, so the office's helping reflexes — more context, the specification, escalation to the author, an improved retry — are each *recorded as the finding* rather than performed, self-directed retry staying legitimate within the run's bound (UIA-3); **experience is depreciation**, vantage-sensitive scenarios taking a fresh instance while the learning, reinforcement and generalization machinery excludes this role in both directions and engagement history is disclosed on every run (UIA-4); knowledge preserved as **findings rather than as the actor's memory**, satisfying ROL-4's never-destroy guarantee by changing where knowledge lives instead of excepting it (UIA-5); capability **matched to the persona, never maximized**, since the office default of the strongest tier portrays a different person and silently removes the finding (UIA-6); one role and many costumes — author, newcomer, host integrator, administrator, returning user — because a role per persona is the fragmentation ROL-9 forbids (UIA-7); **not trusted to self-report but observed independently**, the actor supplying citations into a record it did not write, testimony about one's own confusion being the least reliable kind (UIA-8); independent of whoever built the subject, one rung below DW-9 review neutrality on the ladder of deprivation (UIA-9); reports and never repairs (UIA-10). Carries the four-rung deprivation ladder, the eight-axis inversion table answering ORI-1 against `test-writer`, and an explicit two-half ROL-9 clearing argument (tech-agnostic) | Stable | 1 | 1.0.0 |

- **Prior**: 2026-09-04 (v1.0.187 — **diagram-artifact and evidence-currency wave** via `/reference-mining` from a reference that is a diagram-generation skill: a typed JSON specification, five renderers, a validator with an enumerated fix vocabulary, a three-class delivery-evidence contract, and 46 written rounds of visual-design research standing in for the commit churn the vendored copy has no history for. Operator note steered the pass toward diagram construction. Two new Stable L1s. `l1-diagram-authoring` (DGM-1…DGM-12): the **generated explanatory diagram as an artifact of record** — the object between a live projection (OVZ), a token contract (DI), and a host rendition (HNR), authored once and read by someone who cannot interrogate it, whose failure modes are therefore all silent and all look like competence. Its sharpest three: **a claimed relation lives in the topology, never only in an annotation** (a card saying "retry" is not a transition, a boundary is not a relationship); **labels are content under a declared repair precedence** where deleting one is not a layout repair, and an omission is recorded as a semantic decision because its end state is identical to a silent geometry repair; and **a scoped view names its scope inside the artifact**, since the qualifying prose is lost on the first re-share and the image is what travels. `l1-evidence-currency` (EVC-1…EVC-9): whether evidence describes **the bytes now in hand** and whether a status came from **the run that happened** — the property CMP, TR, and AT each presuppose and none states. Its decisive case is **an output path is not an output**: a failed producing step preserves the previous trusted artifact, so inspecting the path yields a genuine, passing, entirely irrelevant result — the most convincing false pass a pipeline can generate, because every component in it worked correctly. Alongside it, **a failure is never recorded as a skip** (laundering always flows toward the value that raises no question, so that value gets the narrowest definition) and **a pass freezes its subject** (the edits that void a receipt are the ones the author thought were improvements). Six refinements: AO-12/13 (a measurable criterion names its counterfeits and its repair precedence; a threshold belongs to a class, and an item whose measure is undefined for its class is excluded rather than scored), LG-12 (termination on a best-so-far objective, not only on a count), ATE-14 (a diagnostic carries subject, measurement, and admissible fixes; a computed corrective value is used, not re-derived), DC-12 (silence is lenient, declaration is strict, and a declaration may not be withdrawn to pass), IEC-12 (a tiered instruction set names the trigger that opens each tier), CP-10 (reader-facing provenance — foreign text is quoted or replaced, never paraphrased into the product's voice). No Nodus lens this pass: the reference's subject is a rendered artifact, and its one language-shaped mechanic — a typed specification validated before render — is already NL-1/NL-4/NL-20 and NL-28.)
- **Prior**: 2026-09-04 (v1.0.186 — **suppression and ownership wave** via `/reference-mining` from a reference that is a complete Linux desktop distribution with a first-class agent layer: an agent-launching command, per-provider usage collectors, a crash-diagnosis skill, a shipped skill teaching an agent to operate the machine, and roughly nine tenths of its surface outside this product's domain. Two new Stable L1s claiming objects the corpus explicitly deferred or never named. `l1-signal-silencing` (SIL-1…SIL-10): the alerting layer FL-9 hands off in one clause and nothing specifies — a silence withholds delivery and never observation, its key is resolved against real occurrences before acceptance because a phantom silence is indistinguishable from a working one, its breadth is disclosed in the person's own terms, it carries its own reversal and a bound, it is never offered in place of a reachable fix, punch-through rests on attributes the sender cannot profitably forge, what it withheld stays reviewable, escalation survives it, and instance/rule/global stay three controls. `l1-fault-ownership` (OWN-1…OWN-10): the verdict an assembled system produces before any repair — a closed owner set whose *undetermined* value must not collapse into *ours*, a sphere of control enumerated in advance with our configuration of a third party inside it, environment excluded before any component is blamed, a foreign fault routed rather than absorbed, evidence and inference kept separable, unresolvable evidence degrading the claim's grain instead of being completed by invention, diagnosis read-only, and a live-process capture routed deliberately **around** the evidence archive. Eight refinements: AG-9 (a gate is only a gate where the answer can be given), ERR-9 (the diagnostic reaches the surface that triggered the act), REA-12 (absent / unconfigured / unreachable are three verdicts, and a declaration marker is none of them), LAY-11 (a failed layer is never an empty one), CMP-11 (producing the evidence is not examining it), ADO-12/13/14 (window-anchored expiry, a countless source as a bound, an authoritative figure beside the derived one), EM-11 (a record-attached effect travels as data, never as a callback), ORI-12 (the duplicate search covers withdrawn members and is keyed on features, not phrasing). Nodus lens run separately: NL-27/NL-28 in `l1-nodus-language` 1.18.0. The reference's per-field config overlay was **consciously not taken** — LAY-2 forbids deep merge and DC-11 already owns the per-field case at the configuration contract, so the corpus has resolved it in both directions at the right two grains.)

- **Prior**: 2026-09-01 (v1.0.181 - **dispatch-authority and remote-liveness wave** via `/magic.spec` from a re-mining pass over a reference already mined once (a shipping desktop multi-agent orchestrator; twin of the earlier commissioned-execution wave's reference on the review-loop half, but a distinct product) — its flagship best-of-N feature was absorbed last time into `l1-competitive-execution`; this pass worked through the six candidates flagged unmined then, cross-checking each against the corpus as it now stands rather than trusting the prior summary. Two were found already covered: the execution-host-as-addressable- dimension candidate by `l1-execution-locus` + `l1-environment-lifecycle` + `l1-doctor` (all four of precondition/output/terminal-states/participant matched); the self-driving-CLI candidate turned out to be the same control-plane mechanism the new remote-liveness spec already names, not a separate one. Two yielded genuinely new L1 concepts. New `l1-remote-liveness-verdict` (RLV-1...RLV-8, RFC, concept-only) closes a question no existing spec answers: when the only access to a remote entity is a channel that can fail independently of the entity, is it alive, gone, or simply unreachable right now — a closed three-state verdict where *unverifiable* is structurally distinct and never collapsible, *confirmed-gone* requires positive evidence sourced from the party that actually holds the resource (a timeout is never that evidence, regardless of what field it is written into), correlated silence across every entity behind one channel is diagnosed once at the channel rather than multiplied into independent deaths, and one physical resource carries exactly one liveness identity at a time. New `l1-cooperative-status-projection` (ASP-1...ASP-6, RFC, concept-only) closes the adjacent but distinct problem of normalizing many vendor-foreign coding-agent CLIs' incomplete status hooks into one feed — a declared, per-(vendor, transition) inference fallback, generation-fenced so a delayed same-turn signal never resurrects a settled verdict, and addressing authority earned only by a verified live transfer, never by mere persistence or registration. Both closed the same recurring pattern the reference reinforced three separate times (execution-host liveness, dispatch capability tokens, and agent-status hooks): a verdict or authority bound to a specific generation is never superseded by a signal that does not name that generation — generalized from `l1-work-liveness` WL-2's compare-and-clear at the work-claim grain. Two refinements: `l1-orchestration` v1.3.0 (ORC-13 a handoff release ends orchestration bookkeeping without touching the delegate's still-running process, which continues as an explicitly unsupervised entity; ORC-14 dispatch authority as a minted, generation-bound grant revoked exactly once at the first terminal transition, composing `l1-consent-binding` CB-3's bound-element-change rule for inter-agent delegation) and `l1-peer-compatibility` v1.1.0 (PCO-15 — a content or meaning change on a structurally unchanged wire surface is a compatibility break the structural-fingerprint checker cannot see by construction, since nothing about the shape moved; declared on the contract itself as a reviewed claim, the same way PCO-11 already declares emitter behavior no diff can see). One candidate (automation precheck + selective rerun) was found too shallow to formalize beyond what `l1-automation-pipeline` AP-3/AP-6 already require generically. A dedicated Nodus lens found nothing to add at the language grain: `l1-nodus-language` NL-12's correlation-token/deadline pair already realizes the generation-fencing principle at the DSL's own level, and the orchestrator's unsupervised-handoff disposition was deliberately NOT ported to nodus's `RUN`/recursion model, since it would break the bounded-recursion cost-rollup guarantee NL-18 already commits to.)
- **Prior**: 2026-09-01 (v1.0.180 - **commissioned-execution wave** via `/magic.spec` from a reference that is a small cross-model plan-hardening skill collection: one agent locks intent with the user, a *rival vendor's* agent attacks the locked plan read-only round after round, then the two swap jobs to build it, on the doctrine that whoever made a thing never checks it. Almost all of its review-loop core was already owned - producer/grader independence (IR-2, CV-5 including the same-model-lineage reduced-confidence rule), bounded convergence with an honest non-convergent exit (IR-5), frozen criteria (IR-6), the audit trace (IR-7/DL-4/OL), the human gate (RC), a dismissal needing the same evidence as a claim (CMP-7), and a delegate's self-report never relayed as completion (CMP-3/CMP-8/AO-6). What it *did* own alone was the mechanics of **driving another vendor's agent as a subordinate executor**, which nothing covered: the marketplace governs what comes in, host-native rendering what goes out, artifact-derived observation what is read afterwards out-of-band - and none of them the act in the middle. New `l1-foreign-agent-invocation` (FAI-1...FAI-13) names that third direction around one property: **a foreign runtime's failures are predominantly silent and its deviant paths do not default the way its primary path advertises**, so a continuation silently inherits a wider containment posture than the launch it resumes, a process waiting on an unterminated input stalls forever at zero load, a continuation addressed by *most recent* returns a coherent result about different work, and a caller's default deadline kills real work and misreports it as the work's failure - each shaped exactly like success. Three refinements followed: `l1-facilitation` v1.3.0 (FC-14 stake tiering inside FC-13's frontier with the consequence line as the **admission test** - the mechanical fix for the low-value-question defect FC-13 names and leaves unnamed; FC-15 bulk acceptance as a granted delegation over a *named, resolved, marked* set rather than an inference from silence; FC-16 the resolved fact set presented once, sourced, a correction re-opening the question), `l1-capability-reachability` v1.1.0 (REA-11 reachability as a **(capability, executor)** property, established in the invocation mode it will actually run in), and `l1-parallel-staffing` v1.1.0 (PS-10 execution tier assigned per partition class, never inherited from the coordinator, since inheritance multiplies the coordinator's rate by the fan width over characteristically shallow work). Nodus took its own pass: `l1-nodus-language` v1.17.0 NL-25 generated-value branch contract - the point where the language's provenance discipline finally reaches **control flow** rather than content - and NL-26 the deadline on a suspended external step.)
- **Prior**: 2026-08-26 (v1.0.179 — **pre-requirements planning follow-up** via `/magic.spec`, a targeted second pass over the same external engineering-practice toolkit after the user flagged its large-effort mapping practice specifically. The finding that justified a spec rather than a refinement: TG-1 states a task graph is *generated from an explicit requirements artifact*, and **nothing owned the phase where no such artifact exists and none can yet be written** — so that phase was being run through an execution-shaped planner, which satisfies the precondition by inventing scope. New `l1-exploratory-planning` (XPL-1…12): a phase whose **output is decisions, not deliverables**, whose completion tell is the **urge to build**; a **named destination that fixes the scope**; a decision unit completing on a recorded answer; **deliberate incompleteness** with the admission test being *can the question be stated sharply now*, not *can it be answered* (sharp-but-blocked is a unit, not-yet-sharp is fog, and pre-slicing fog manufactures precision that does not exist); **out-of-scope as terminal and distinct from fog**, kept out of the decisions record because a scope boundary is not a step on the walked route; map-as-index; claim-before-work with the frontier defined as open + unblocked + **unclaimed**; and a **handoff obligation** that collapses the map into TG-1's artifact rather than feeding units straight to an executor. The load-bearing invariant reaches past planning: **XPL-4** — a unit needing a human is never completed by the actor supplying the human's side, an actor that asks its own questions and answers them having fabricated the counterparty. Three refinements: **TG-15** (generation refused rather than improvised when the source artifact is absent or substantially unsettled, naming the upstream phase), **CONV-9** (a decision-shaped unit converges on the one board by construction, closing the off-board planning loophole that is CONV-1's shadow work wearing a phase name), **CMP-10** (the general form of XPL-4: a completion requiring a counterparty is not satisfied by the actor's own contribution — the class every one of CMP-1…CMP-9 misses, because the work was real and the report honest while the *participant* was fabricated))
- **Prior**: 2026-08-26 (v1.0.178 — **working-context and standing-instruction wave** via `/magic.spec` from a reference that is a composable set of engineering practices for coding agents, built as small adaptable units rather than a process that owns the workflow. Five new Stable L1s claiming grains the corpus specified transforms for and never made decisions about. `l1-context-transition` (CXT-1…12): the token-economy family specifies *how* each transform works and never *which one, when* — a cost-ordered ladder (continue / discard / externalize / delegate / compress) evaluated **only at a work boundary**, with the primary→secondary source conversion named as the thing that loses the *why*, a threshold that signals but never fires, discard requiring positive evidence of irrelevance rather than absence of a reason to keep, and compression as the default that is never the first reach. `l1-capability-reachability` (REA-1…10): **who may invoke a capability** — agent-reachable versus human-only as a closed partition, with a person's **recall treated as a budget spent deliberately rather than minimized**, the class test being autonomy rather than reuse, human-only capabilities structurally uncomposable (so shared material lives in neither), and a stale router graded a correctness defect. `l1-rejection-memory` (REJ-1…12): the durable record of declined proposals, keyed by concept and consulted at generation time, with the two contamination guards stated as invariants — a **deferral is not a rejection**, and **already-satisfied is not rejected**. `l1-instruction-economy` (IEC-1…11): the sentence grain below harness composition and reachability — a line earns its place by changing behaviour against the **actor's own default**, a verdict that is measured by running rather than argued and **expires when the actor changes**, plus documentation-as-cache, scattering versus duplication, placement by context pressure, and prohibition-raises-what-it-forbids. `l1-concept-grounding` (GRD-1…10): the dependency structure inside any artifact read in order — assumed versus established, **the concept and not its name**, the grounded set carried with the position (which is what reconciles ordered artifacts with order-independent production), and termination by destination rather than by exhausting the source. Six refinements: RC-11/RC-12 (latest-reversible checkpoint placement; a decision-ready brief that *reaches* the artifact), FC-13 (frontier-batched elicitation with recommended answers, and the fact/decision split, reconciling the apparent tension with FC-4), NEG-11 (the positive target is the primary form an exclusion takes at the generator), CTN-12 (wide mechanical change sequenced expand→migrate→contract), ES-21 (a generated evaluation item never leaks its answer through surface form), CE-11 (design contests assign each variant a declared constraint, and settle by deliberation rather than by CE's one-whole-attempt rule). Cross-workspace: nodus `l1-nodus-dialog` v1.4.0 (DG-11 latest-reversible dialog placement and a decision-ready payload carrying a reference rather than inlined content))
- **Earlier**: 2026-08-20 (v1.0.174 — **evidence-archive wave** via `/magic.spec` from a design scheme placing a never-deleted, never-uploaded raw local record beneath a compaction layer that stores exact ranges rather than replacing them. New `l1-evidence-archive` (EA-1…EA-12) claims the raw non-numeric evidentiary plane no spec owned, and inverts the destructive default: the system's automatic compaction becomes an indexing operation while destruction becomes an explicit act. - **Earlier**: 2026-08-20 (v1.0.172 — **inverse-derivation pair** via `/magic.spec` from a design session on learning construction method from exemplary work rather than from the office's own sessions. Two new Stable L1s forming a supplier→consumer pair with a deliberately narrow, one-directional seam. `l1-inverse-derivation` (IVD-1…13): running the production arrow backwards over work the office did not produce, on the **episode** grain — a completed arc yielding a recoverable before-state, an after-state, executable checks, and a trajectory — with every recovered link graded **attested / inferred / assumed** and grades non-inheriting, the **trajectory as first-class output** on the grounds that the artifact already shows the answer and only the record shows the search, an explicit **pinned and fingerprinted** corpus registry closing the silent-drift hole where a rewritten upstream history invalidates every measurement with no error reported, discovery permitted to propose and forbidden to admit, and selection on **record quality rather than product popularity** since proxy metrics rank product success and would systematically acquire the un-reconstructable. `l1-capability-leveling` (LVL-1…17): the closed replay loop whose objective is **spread collapse at a bar, never reproduction**, terminating on a measured two-part marginal rule that replaces the non-terminating "iterate until identical"; comparison over outcome **and trajectory**, with an implausibly straight path recorded as a **contamination signal rather than a success** — the structural answer to a framing that, being reconstructed from finished work, already knows the answer; four-way divergence classification with **arbitrary** discarded under an explicit retention bias; **carrier chosen by who executes the lesson** across a five-rung ladder, since filing every lesson as instruction places it in the highest-variance carrier for the problem defined by execution variance; hardening refused where it degrades strong performers; and a **single human ratification gate on the assembled body of lessons** at the boundary, with the loop feeding the existing distillation machinery rather than standing up a second store.)
- **Earlier**: 2026-08-19 (v1.0.171 — **plugin-architecture wave** via `/magic.spec` from a reference that is a plugin-framework-based agent harness whose every part, including its model adapter and its agent loop, is a replaceable plugin. Four new Stable L1s covering the three dimensions the existing extension model never reached — visibility, binding, and world. `l1-scoped-capability-layers` (SCL-1…13): one registry with per-actor layers, the registration site driving visibility and lifetime as one fact, and one chain read in opposite directions (supply down, observation up); with scoping declared **not** an authority boundary, because a mechanism shaped like isolation is used as isolation unless it says otherwise. `l1-composition-binding` (CBD-1…13): a binding names a **generation**, so a running actor outlives its composition file being edited or deleted, a child joins the parent's exact generation by reference rather than re-resolving its name, and every reconstruction resolves *runs-on* rather than reading the frozen *started-with*. `l1-composition-layering` (LAY-1…10): author-assigned entry identity as the sole reconciliation basis, whole replacement with no deep merge, one composition algorithm serving both the run path and every inspection surface, and ownership decided structurally — exact match to the shipped form is system-owned, any deviation user-owned. `l1-execution-locus` (LOC-1…10): the locus as the **unit of substitution, never the individual capability**, closing the previously-flagged execution-host dimension — a shell in one world and a file reader in another produce tools that individually succeed and jointly describe an impossible world. Five amendments: IB-13 (deferred satisfaction legitimate until a declared settlement point, a startup failure after it, enumerating per unit the needs never supplied), INT-11 (declared dispatch discipline, machine-checked against the dispatch site), EP-13 (registration as a reversible effect whose teardown reaches quiescence rather than requesting it), WL-10 (where intake is shared, a result belongs to an interval and never to a message), and nodus LP-22 (a run pinned to the definition generation it validated against).)
